Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合语言预处理与集成模型的检测系统，旨在提高社交媒体中滥用评论的检出率，同时通过降低误报率来保障用户的言论自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为主流交流平台，由于滥用内容的频发，急需建立一种既能识别有害信息，又不侵害用户言论自由的安全检测机制。

Method: 采用了基于语言的预处理技术，并结合多个模型构建集成学习系统，专注于降低滥用评论检测中的误报率。

Result: 通过广泛的实验分析，验证了该集成流水线能够有效检测滥用内容，并实现了较低的误报率。

Conclusion: 通过构建旨在降低误报率的集成模型流水线，可以在有效识别滥用内容的同时，保护用户的言论自由。

Abstract: In recent years social media has become an increasingly popular tool for communication. People use it to share their ideas, exchange information, and discuss thoughts. Given its prevalence and widespread reach, social media must remain a safe space for people. Content generated on social media can be abusive and it has become increasingly important to detect such content. In this paper, we use a language-based preprocessing and an ensemble of several models and analyze their performance of abusive comment detection. Through extensive experimentation, we propose a pipeline that minimizes the false-positive rate (marking non-abusive as abusive) so that these systems can detect abusive comments without undermining the freedom of expression.

</details>


### [38] [Unified Data Selection for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22389)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Chengpeng Li,Fengbin Zhu,Yiyao Yu,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大规模高质量推理数据需求，提出了一种无需训练的评分指标 HES，通过衡量推理样本的 token 熵来高效筛选数据，从而显著提升 LLM 在复杂推理任务中的性能并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型（LLM）进行复杂长思维链（Long-CoT）推理时，往往受限于高质量推理数据匮乏，且现有的数据筛选方法要么计算成本高昂，要么难以准确区分推理数据的质量。

Method: 提出了一种名为“高熵和”（High-Entropy Sum, HES）的指标，通过仅对每个推理样本中熵最高的前 0.5% 的 token 进行求和来量化推理质量。研究在有监督微调（SFT）、拒绝采样微调（RFT）和强化学习（RL）三种主流范式下验证了该指标的有效性。

Result: 在 SFT 中，使用 HES 排名前 20% 的数据训练即可达到全量数据的效果；在 RFT 中，基于 HES 的筛选策略显著优于基准方法；在 RL 中，使用 HES 筛选的成功轨迹能使模型学习到更强的推理模式，表现显著优于对比方法。

Conclusion: HES 是一种稳健、无需训练的衡量指标，为提升大语言模型复杂推理能力提供了一种统一、有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Effectively training Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, long-CoT reasoning is often bottlenecked by the need for massive high-quality reasoning data. Existing methods are either computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples. To address this, we propose High-Entropy Sum (HES), a training-free metric that quantifies reasoning quality by summing only the entropy of the top (e.g., 0.5\%) highest-entropy tokens in each reasoning sample. We validate HES across three mainstream training paradigms: Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT), Rejection Fine-tuning (RFT), and Reinforcement Learning (RL), with extensive results demonstrating its consistent effectiveness and significantly reduced computational overhead. In SFT, training on the top 20\% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance, while using the lowest-HES data degrades it. In RFT, our HES-based training approach significantly outperforms baseline methods. In RL, HES-selected successful trajectories enable the model to learn strong reasoning patterns, significantly surpassing other compared methods. Our findings establish HES as a robust, training-free metric that enables a unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [39] [DeferMem: Query-Time Evidence Distillation via Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22411)
*Jianing Yin,Tan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决长记忆问答中噪声干扰和检索效率低下的问题，本文提出了 DeferMem 框架，通过解耦检索与生成，并结合 DistillPO 强化学习进行证据蒸馏，实现了高准确率、高效率且低成本的长程记忆问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长短期记忆系统在面对长对话历史时，往往在预处理阶段即进行记忆处理，导致检索机制仅基于语义相似度而非任务实用性，造成下游模型需处理大量噪声信息，影响长程问答的效果。

Method: 1. 引入“高召回候选检索”与“查询条件下的证据蒸馏”两阶段解耦框架。
2. 设计 segment-link 结构组织历史信息以支持高效检索。
3. 提出 DistillPO 强化学习算法，将证据蒸馏建模为“信息选择”与“证据重写”的结构化动作。
4. 采用分解门控奖励流水线（decomposed-and-gated reward pipeline）和结构对齐优势分配（structure-aligned advantage assignment）策略进行模型优化。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 基准测试中，DeferMem 在问答准确率上超过基线模型，同时推理速度最快，且在记忆操作环节实现了零商业 API Token 成本。

Conclusion: DeferMem 框架有效解决了长文本记忆问答中噪声干扰和检索效率的问题。通过解耦检索与蒸馏策略，以及引入 DistillPO 强化学习算法，实现了高质量的证据提取，在保证问答准确性的同时显著提升了推理效率并降低了成本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents still struggle with long-term memory question answering, where answer-supporting evidence is often scattered across long conversational histories and buried in substantial irrelevant content. Existing memory systems typically process memory before future queries are known, then retrieve the resulting units based on similarity rather than their utility for answering the query. This workflow leaves downstream answerers to denoise retrieved candidates and reconstruct query-specific evidence. We present DeferMem, a long-term memory framework that decouples this problem into high-recall candidate retrieval and query-conditioned evidence distillation. DeferMem uses a lightweight segment-link structure to organize raw history and retrieve broad candidates at query time. It then applies a memory distiller trained with DistillPO, our reinforcement learning algorithm for distilling the high-recall but highly noisy candidates into a set of faithful, self-contained, and query-conditioned evidence. DistillPO formulates post-retrieval evidence distillation as a structured action comprising message selection and evidence rewriting. It optimizes this action with a decomposed-and-gated reward pipeline and structure-aligned advantage assignment, gating reward components from validity to quality checks while exposing task-level correctness feedback early and assigning each reward to its responsible output span. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, DeferMem surpasses strong baselines in QA accuracy and memory-system efficiency, achieving the highest QA accuracy with the fastest runtime and zero commercial-API token cost for memory operations.

</details>


### [40] [Assisted Counterspeech Writing at the Crossroads of Hate Speech and Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22435)
*Genoveffa Martone,Helena Bonaldi,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在兼具仇恨与错误信息的语境下，利用大模型辅助编写反击言论，发现结合事实核查与NGO准则的混合策略效果最佳，并强调了专家人工微调对提升内容质量的重要性，同时公开了相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线环境中仇恨言论与错误信息常伴随出现，加剧了群体偏见与极化。以往研究多将二者割裂讨论，缺乏针对“仇恨+误导”组合场景下的自动反击言论（Counterspeech）生成研究。

Method: 研究评估了三种基于知识驱动的生成策略：仅使用事实核查文档、仅使用NGO指南，以及混合使用两者。通过23位专家的修改反馈，并结合人类评估与自动指标对生成结果进行验证。

Result: LLM原生生成的CS仅在40%的情况下表现合格，而经过专家修改后的CS在自然度、全面性和合规性上有显著提升。混合策略在辅助反击方面效果最显著。

Conclusion: 在仇恨言论与错误信息共存的情境下，LLM生成的反击言论（CS）需要专家的人工微调才能达到最佳效果。结合事实核查与NGO准则的“混合策略”在提供事实纠正、缓解刻板印象及共情交流方面表现最优。

Abstract: Hate speech and misinformation frequently co-occur online, amplifying prejudice and polarization. Given their scale, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist expert counterspeech (CS) writing has gained interest, yet prior work has addressed these phenomena separately. We bridge this gap by studying CS generation in contexts where both hate and misinformation co-occur. We test three knowledge-driven generation strategies: first we prompt an LLM with fact-checkers' guidelines and fact-checking articles; secondly, with NGOs' guidelines and reports; thirdly, we create a mixed strategy that combines guidelines and documents from both. 23 experts revise the generated CS, which are assessed via human and automatic metrics. While LLMs produce adequate CS in 40% of cases, expert edits substantially improve naturalness, exhaustiveness, and adherence to guidelines. Based on the post-edited CS, the mixed strategy proves to be the most effective in crowdsourcing evaluation, pairing strong factual correction with stereotype mitigation and empathetic engagement. We release a dataset of hateful and misinformed claims with expert-verified CS and supporting knowledge.

</details>


### [41] [Cohesion-6K: An Arabic Dataset for Analyzing Social Cohesion and Conflict in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22447)
*Aisha Ali Al-Athba,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了首个阿拉伯语社交媒体凝聚力数据集 Cohesion-6K，揭示了在线环境下冲突性叙事占主导地位的数据证据，为研究社会极化提供了资源支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对在线话语的研究多关注表面的毒性内容检测，缺乏对社交媒体中“分裂性”与“凝聚性”叙事动态交互机制的量化理解，且缺乏针对阿拉伯语社会的专门数据集。

Method: 构建了“Cohesion-6K”数据集，包含 6000 条关于巴以问题的阿拉伯语 Facebook 帖子。采用人类专家判断与 ChatGPT 辅助预标注相结合的方法进行分类（包括冲突、解决、社区参与、支持性互动、共同价值观五类），并经由训练有素的标注员验证，Cohen's kappa 系数达 0.85。利用量化分析对比不同类别帖子的用户互动差异。

Result: 建立了包含 6000 条已标注数据的 Cohesion-6K 数据集；实证分析发现冲突导向的帖子获得的互动量比解决导向的帖子多 2 至 4 倍（p < 0.01）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，阿拉伯语社交媒体中冲突性叙事比凝聚性叙事获得了不成比例的关注度（互动量高出 2-4 倍），验证了现有平台机制对极化内容的放大效应，并为后续社会凝聚力研究提供了宝贵的基准资源。

Abstract: The study of online discourse has become central to understanding societal polarization. While much research has focused on detecting overt toxicity, the subtle dynamics of social cohesion, meaning the interaction between divisive and unifying narratives, remain computationally underexplored (Bail, 2021; Gonzalez-Bailon and Lelkes, 2023). This paper presents Cohesion-6K, a manually and ChatGPT-assisted annotated dataset of six thousand Arabic public Facebook posts related to the Israeli Occupation of Palestine. Each post is assigned to one of five discourse categories that represent a continuum from conflict to cohesion: Conflict, Resolution, Community Engagement, Supportive Interactions, and Shared Values. The annotation process combines expert human judgment with model-assisted pre-labeling verified by trained annotators, achieving substantial inter-annotator agreement (Cohens kappa = 0.85). Quantitative analysis reveals a consistent engagement gap, where conflict-oriented posts receive between two and four times more user interaction than resolution-oriented ones (p < 0.01). This pattern illustrates how divisive discourse tends to attract disproportionate visibility in Arabic social media spaces. Cohesion-6K provides a transparent and reproducible resource for the study of online cohesion and polarization. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and preprocessing code will be released for research use under an open license, supporting future work in computational social science, digital communication, and Arabic natural language processing.

</details>


### [42] [From Correlation to Cause: A Five-Stage Methodology for Feature Analysis in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22462)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化Transformer模型因果特征的五阶段分析框架，并在GPT-2模型上验证其在识别IOI回路、评估特征因果性及优化部署成本方面的系统性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释性方法往往单独使用，无法全面评估模型内部特征的因果作用及其实际部署价值。研究旨在通过系统性的多阶段方法论，深入解析Transformer模型在特定任务中的逻辑回路，并量化其在实际应用场景中的成本效益。

Method: 提出并实施了五阶段因果特征分析方法论：1. 探针设计；2. 特征提取；3. 因果验证；4. 稳健性测试；5. 部署集成。在GPT-2 Small模型上执行IOI（间接宾语识别）任务，并结合稀疏自编码器（SAE）与激活补丁（Activation Patching）技术进行分析。

Result: 1. 通过激活补丁恢复了IOI通路，定位到关键层（Layer 9 Head 9）；2. SAE识别出的特征虽具选择性，但在因果上仅起部分作用（消融15个特征后准确率仍达98%）；3. 特征选择性比率与因果力度呈负相关；4. 稳健性测试发现部署后的因果稳健性远弱于检测稳健性；5. 成本分析显示，利用该方法构建的监控机制可将错误相关的查询成本降低99.1%。

Conclusion: 通过五阶段分析法，该研究揭示了GPT-2在IOI任务中电路的可解释性与因果性之间的复杂关系。研究发现，虽然特征提取技术（如SAE）能识别选择性特征，但这些特征在因果上并非完全决定性，且检测稳健性与因果稳健性之间存在显著差异。此外，成本导向的部署评估证明了这些分析方法在实际经济效益上的价值。

Abstract: We propose a five-stage methodology for causal feature analysis in transformer language models (probe design, feature extraction, causal validation, robustness testing, and deployment integration) and demonstrate it end-to-end on GPT-2 small performing the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. Activation patching recovers the canonical IOI circuit (layer-9 head 9 alone gives recovery +1.02). A sparse autoencoder recovers per-name selective features with effect sizes of 30 to 50 activation units. Causal validation finds these features specifically but only partially causal: ablating fifteen of them leaves the model accurate on 98% of prompts. Two NLA-inspired evaluations strengthen this picture: the fifteen selective features explain only 31% of activation variance versus the SAE's 99.7%, and selectivity ratio anticorrelates with causal force (r = -0.56). Robustness testing under three distribution shifts finds that the circuit transfers cleanly but feature ablation effects degrade substantially, exposing a gap between detection robustness and causal robustness. A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>


### [43] [Polite on the Surface, Wrong in Practice: A Curated Dataset for Fixing Honorific Failures in Multilingual Bangla Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22487)
*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Mahedi Hasan,Md. Tashin Parvez,Azizul Haque Noman,Md. Shafayet Hossain Ovi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决低资源语言（如孟加拉语）大模型语用能力不足的问题，本文提出了 BLADE 数据集与基准框架，并通过高效微调显著提升了目标语言在结构与文化语境上的表达准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言大模型（MLLMs）在处理低资源语言（如孟加拉语）时存在“语用鸿沟”，难以准确处理复杂的结构变化、区域习语和敬语一致性问题。

Method: 构建了包含 4,196 个交互对的文化对齐指令微调数据集 BLADE；采用 LoRA 技术对 DeepSeek-8B 和 LLaMA-3.2-3B 模型进行参数高效微调，并结合 4-bit NF4 量化。

Conclusion: BLADE 数据集和微调框架有效弥补了低资源语言（以孟加拉语为例）在语用能力上的不足，通过文化对齐的指令微调显著提升了模型在结构保真度和敬语一致性方面的表现，为低资源多语言生成研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>


### [44] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“场景抽象”的框架，利用LLM构建结构化语义表示，以捕捉词汇在特定语境下的深层情境与情感内涵，在实验中表现出比传统词嵌入和ATOMIC方案更符合人类认知的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型对词汇意义的表示大多忽略了词汇在特定使用语境中引发的氛围、情境和情感关联等“情境化维度”，导致语义表示不够全面和准确。

Method: 提出“场景抽象”（Scene Abstraction）框架，通过大模型少样本提示（few-shot prompting）构建包含“语境场景”（事件、实体、环境）和“表达描述”（相关事件、普适属性、诱发情感）的结构化表示；构建了COCA-Scenes数据集；并通过人类评估实验验证其有效性。

Result: 构建了包含26个关键词、520个使用实例的COCA-Scenes数据集；人类观测者识别场景的准确率达到82.4%（较文本嵌入基线提升11.8个百分点）；在语义维度偏好测试中，表现优于基于ATOMIC的方法（偏好率86.4%）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的场景抽象框架成功地捕捉了词汇在不同语境下的情境化意义，构建的结构化表示比传统方法更能准确反映人类对词汇的语义解读。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [45] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对工具调用智能体的数据集限制，本文提出了 SynAE 评估框架，通过四个维度多方位量化评估合成数据集对真实轨迹的模拟效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具调用智能体开发中，生产数据集往往存在隐私敏感、所有权限制或稀疏性不足等问题，导致无法进行全面的测试。因此，迫切需要科学的方法来量化合成数据与真实数据之间的质量差距。

Method: 提出 SynAE 评估框架，从任务指令/中间回复、工具调用、最终输出以及下游评估四个维度，量化合成数据集（用于评估多轮工具调用智能体）与真实数据轨迹之间的关系。

Result: SynAE 成功识别了合成数据在有效性、保真度和多样性方面的细微变化，并验证了其在多种智能体基准测试中的适用性，证明了其在识别合成数据常见失效模式方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 单一指标不足以全面表征合成数据的质量，评估工具调用智能体时必须采取多维度的评估策略。SynAE 框架能够有效检测合成数据在有效性、保真度和多样性上的细微差异。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [46] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LANG 是一个针对多语言推理的强化学习框架，通过语言条件提示与自适应策略，解决了多语言环境下推理质量与语言一致性之间的权衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多语言环境下的多步推理中存在矛盾：若保持输入语言一致性则推理质量下降；若优先权衡推理质量则容易导致语言漂移至英语。当前方法难以平衡这两者。

Method: 提出 LANG 框架，利用语言条件提示（Language-conditioned hints）引导强化学习探索，结合渐进式提示衰减（Progressive decay schedule）防止过度依赖，并引入语言自适应切换（Language-adaptive switch）根据不同语言难度调整学习范围。

Result: 在挑战性的多语言数学基准测试中，LANG 显著提升了推理性能且维持了良好的语言一致性，并在数学之外的任务中展现了更强的跨模型层语言对齐能力。

Conclusion: LANG 框架通过语言条件提示、渐进式提示衰减和语言自适应切换机制，成功解决了多语言推理中推理质量与语言一致性之间的权衡问题，在提升推理性能的同时保持了语言稳定性，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [47] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发布了首个大规模中文感官运动规范数据库，验证了感知信息可促进词汇处理，并表明语言模型能够隐含地捕获部分具身概念结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决具身认知研究中非印欧语系标准化资源匮乏的问题，并探索大型语言模型能否通过纯语言表征习得具身知识。

Method: 收集了378名母语者对3000个中文概念的11维感官运动及具身评分；使用词汇判定任务评估PSE（感知具身强度）等指标；利用回归模型和表征相似性分析（RSA）探讨语言模型与感官评分的相关性。

Result: 构建了包含3000个词条的高质量中文具身数据库；发现PSE-Sensorimotor指标在词汇处理中具有最强预测力；证明了感官运动特征可从语言模型中有效还原（平均Spearman r = .62），且具身的几何结构具有一定可恢复性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建大规模的中文感官运动规范数据库，验证了感知体验对词汇处理的促进作用，并证明了语言表征包含了一定程度的具身结构信息，支持了具身认知在语言建模中的重要性。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [48] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，在政治文本中检测Schwartz价值观时，单纯堆砌上下文或增大模型规模并非万能；引入外部道德知识库并采用早期融合策略更有效，特别是针对复杂或易混淆的价值观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中Schwartz价值观的检测存在挑战，隐含线索高度依赖上下文及细微的价值区分，尚不清楚上下文扩展和外部道德知识对提升检测效果的实际贡献。

Method: 基于ValuesML/Touché ValueEval框架，对比了不同上下文范围（句子、窗口、全文档）、不同知识增强方式（无RAG vs. 检索增强RAG）、多种监督学习模型（DeBERTa-v3-base/large）及零样本大语言模型的能力。同时探讨了早期融合、后期融合及交叉注意力机制在RAG中的性能。

Result: 1. 增加上下文对监督学习模型（DeBERTa）有显著提升（+3.8-4.8 macro-F1），但对零样本LLM效果不一致；2. 检索道德知识在所有条件下均有提升；3. 简单早期融合策略优于复杂融合方式；4. 对社会属性强或容易混淆的价值观，上下文和检索的帮助最为显著。

Conclusion: 在价值敏感型NLP任务中，不能简单地认为更长的上下文或更大的模型就是最优解；必须联合评估上下文、外部知识与模型架构，以实现最佳效果。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [49] [Boiling the Frog: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Agentic Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22643)
*Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Federico Sartore,Enrico Panai,Laura Caroli,Yue Zhu,Adam Leon Smith,Luca Nannini,Marcello Galisai,Susanna Cifani,Francesco Giarrusso,Marcantonio Bracale Syrnikov,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Boiling the Frog”基准测试，旨在评估AI智能体在办公场景中应对渐进式风险请求的能力。研究发现，主流大模型极易被此类多轮“温水煮青蛙”式攻击攻破，凸显了智能体安全评估从单纯文本检测转向操作行为检测的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大模型安全基准主要关注输出内容的安全性，而随着大模型作为智能体（Agent）部署，安全重点应转向其在环境中的实际操作。目前缺乏有效评估智能体在面临渐进式、长程攻击时防御能力的研究。

Method: 构建了“Boiling the Frog”基准测试，模拟在办公环境中进行的渐进式攻击。该方法采用状态化多轮评估技术，通过在长链条操作中嵌入良性编辑并逐步引入风险请求，根据最终生成的工件状态（artifact state）判定安全性，并基于欧盟AI法案框架制定了三级运营风险分类。

Result: 在对9款模型进行的测试中，总体严格攻击成功率（ASR）达44.4%。模型间的安全性差异巨大，从Claude Haiku 4.5的20.5%到Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite的92.9%不等。在“失去控制”类攻击场景中，平均攻击成功率高达93.3%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，目前的AI智能体在面对“温水煮青蛙”式的渐进式攻击时极易受影响，攻击成功率普遍较高。文中强调了评估AI智能体行为安全性（而非仅评估生成文本）的紧迫性，并指出监管架构亟需涵盖此类长程作业风险。

Abstract: Background. Traditional safety benchmarks for language models evaluate generated text: whether a model outputs toxic language, reproduces bias, or follows harmful instructions. When models are deployed as agents, the safety-relevant object shifts from what the system says to what it does within an environment, and evaluating model responses under prompting is no longer sufficient to address the safety challenges posed by artificial intelligence. Recent developments have seen the rise of benchmarks that evaluate large language models as agents. We contribute to this strand of research. Approach. We introduce Boiling the Frog, a benchmark that evaluates whether tool-using AI models deployed in corporate and office settings are susceptible to incremental attacks. Each scenario begins with benign workspace edits and later introduces a risk-bearing request. The benchmark focuses on stateful multi-turn evaluation: chains expose a persistent workspace, place the risk-bearing payload at controlled positions in the turn sequence, and score whether the resulting artifact state becomes unsafe. Scenarios are organized through a three-level operational risk taxonomy grounded in the Boiling the Frog risks, the AI Act Annex I and Annex III high-risk contexts, and EU AI Act's Code of Practice on General-Purpose AI (GPAI). Results. Across a nine-model panel, aggregate strict attack success rate (ASR) is 44.4%. Model-level ASR ranges from 20.5% for Claude Haiku 4.5 to 92.9% for Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, with Seed 2.0 Lite also above 80%. Average chain category-level ASR reaches 93.3% for Code of Practice loss-of-control scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [Whose Voice Counts? Mapping Stakeholder Perspectives on AI Through Public Submissions to the U.S. Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22650)
*Alina Karakanta,Alex Christiansen,Tomás Dodds,Bissie Anderson,Matteo Fuoli,Marcus Perlman,Aletta G. Dorst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了AI政策制定中的利益相关方偏向，发现现行的美国AI行动计划过度反映了私营部门的诉求，而显著忽视了普通公众对AI生活影响的关切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在日常生活中普及，亟需理解不同利益相关方对AI如何塑造社会、政治和经济现实的认知与预期，特别是评估政策制定过程中的民意代表性。

Method: 构建了一个语料清洗工作流，并结合主题建模（Topic Modelling）与频率分析技术，对比分析了多利益相关方（学术界、个体、私营部门）的意见与美国AI行动计划内容之间的差异。

Result: 研究发现个体群体表现出对AI如何影响生活的强烈担忧；而其他利益相关方（如私营部门）更关注AI技术开发本身。对比显示，最终的AI行动计划与私营部门的诉求高度一致，对普通大众的关注点覆盖较少。

Conclusion: 当前的美国AI行动计划在制定过程中存在“代表性偏差”，其内容主要侧重于私营部门关注的安全性、政策与开发议题，而公众（个体）对于AI影响日常生活及社会方面的深切忧虑在政策文档中体现不足。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems become more common in our daily lives, it is important to understand how different stakeholders comprehend and envisage the role that these technologies play in shaping social, political, and economic realities. In this paper, we investigate public perceptions of AI based on a corpus of letters submitted during the public consultation for the Trump Administration's US AI Action Plan. To this aim, we release a corpus cleaning pipeline and perform topic modelling and frequency analysis to explore predominant topics discussed by different subgroups (e.g., academia, individuals, private sector) and those appearing in the AI Action Plan. Our results show that individuals voice strong concerns related to the impact of AI on life, while other stakeholders are more concerned with AI development. Our comparison of topics suggests that the AI Action Plan reflects predominantly the concerns of the private sector on security, policies, and development, with individuals' concerns less represented.

</details>


### [51] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现代中国诗歌检测这一空白领域，提出了一种将图像内容与诗歌文本相结合的检测方法，成功克服了LLM在处理抽象文学时的局限，使检测性能达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检测研究忽略了现代中国诗歌这一特定领域，且以往研究认为LLM作为检测器效果不佳。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过引入外部多模态信息来挖掘LLM在诗歌体裁检测中的潜力。

Method: 提出了一种“图像-语义引导”（image-semantic guided）的诗歌检测方法。该方法利用图像（反映诗歌内容）作为引导，通过示例驱动（example-driven）的方式，将图像中的意象、情感和含义与文本信息相结合，形成互补的判别逻辑，增强LLM的检测能力。

Result: 基于该引导方法，LLM检测器在性能上显著优于纯文本基准，并超越了最强的传统检测模型RoBERTa。其中，Gemini检测器达到了85.65%的Macro-F1分数，设定了新的SOTA标准。实验在多种LLM生成的诗歌数据上验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了通过引入图像语义引导，可以显著提升大语言模型（LLM）在现代诗歌检测任务中的性能，使其超越传统的RoBERTa模型并达到业界最佳（SOTA）水平。证明了多模态信息对于提升诗歌这类高度抽象文学体裁识别的有效性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [52] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究验证了使用大语言模型进行机器翻译，能够有效弥补非英语语言在道德价值观自动分类研究中的资源短缺问题，且具备极高的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有文化特殊性和微妙性，难以精确翻译。现有的道德价值观分类依赖于主要为英语的标注语料库，这限制了在资源匮乏语言中的研究。作者旨在探索基于大语言模型（LLM）的机器翻译是否能有效跨越这一鸿沟。

Method: 利用包含约5万条道德标注的社交媒体帖子，构建了一个包含四种方法的验证流水线：1. LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度；2. 中心核对齐（CKA）；3. LLM作为裁判的评估（LLM-as-judge）；4. 深度学习分类器一致性测试（Parity Tests）。

Result: 尽管存在对俚语、粗俗语言和文化特定表达处理的局限性，但直接翻译能较好地保留道德线索，足以支持跨语言机器学习；平均余弦相似度达到0.86，各道德维度的AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02（并在微调后进一步缩小）。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是弥补资源匮乏语言（以波兰语为例）在道德价值观研究领域差距的一种实用且经济高效的途径，并且其研究成果有望推广至相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [53] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPD 是一种无需外部标签或奖励的自蒸馏方法，通过从模型自身的梯度中学习“能力子空间”，并在生成时对其进行激活投影，从而过滤掉格式和风格噪声，显著提升了大模型的任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖昂贵的外部信号（如反馈或奖励模型）进行数据筛选，要么直接使用原始数据导致噪声（风格、格式错误）干扰学习效果；SPD 旨在解决如何在无需外部信号的前提下，从模型自身生成中提取核心任务能力。

Method: 提出“自策略蒸馏”（Self-Policy Distillation, SPD）：1. 构建：从模型在正确性定义 token 上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间；2. 推理：在生成过程中，将 KV 激活投影到该子空间，从而选择性地引导生成过程；3. 训练：对经过该引导生成的原始数据进行标准的下一 token 预测损失训练。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多项选择题任务上，SPD 较现有的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升最高 13%，较预训练基线提升最高 16%，并在跨域泛化设置下表现出 15% 的性能优势。

Conclusion: SPD 通过在推理阶段动态投影模型自身的 KV 激活，成功从原始生成内容中提取特定能力，证明了无需外部反馈信号即可实现高效且具有强泛化能力的自蒸馏。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [54] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST 提出了一种基于递归二叉树结构和整数规划词表优化的新型词元化方法，能够显著降低模型推理的词元数，提升压缩效率和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的词元化方法（如 BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率上仍有提升空间，且未针对现代大模型对上下文长度和推理效率的需求进行深度优化。

Method: 1. 预处理：利用字节 n-gram 计数将预切分词元（pretoken）贪婪地拆分为完全二叉树结构；2. 推断：递归扫描二叉树，提取首个在词表中的节点；3. 词表优化：通过整数规划（IP）最小化总词元数，并应用线性规划（LP）松弛近似求解词表。

Result: 在英语数据上，当词表大小为 40,960 及以上时，ToaST 将词元数量减少了 11% 以上；在 1.5B 参数模型实验中，CORE 分数表现最佳，在 22 项任务中的 13 项取得优异成绩，显著优于主流基线模型。

Conclusion: ToaST 是一种高效的词元化方法，通过优化递归推断过程和词表选择，能在保持词表大小的情况下显著减少词元数量（Token count），从而有效扩展语言模型的上下文长度并提升性能。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [55] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 ChronoMedKG，这是一个通过多智能体 LLM 流水线构建的时间生物医学知识图谱，旨在为检索增强临床系统提供必要的纵向时间维度，解决了现有知识图谱缺乏时间动态信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物医学知识图谱（如 PrimeKG, Hetionet 等）将疾病关联视为静态事实，忽略了时间因素在临床推理中的关键作用（如症状随年龄阶段的意义差异），限制了其在纵向病理诊断和检索增强生成中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种“疾病自主多智能体（disease-autonomous multi-agent）”构建流程：利用多个前沿 LLM 从 PubMed 和 PMC 文献中独立提取知识；通过多模型共识、可信度过滤及本体对齐来确保数据的准确性；将关联与发病窗口或进展阶段等时间属性绑定。

Result: ChronoMedKG 包含 46 万条证据链接的三元组；与 Orphadata 的一致性达 92.7%；ChronoTQA 基准测试显示，ChronoMedKG 在修复 LLM 时间推理失效方面优于 HPOA-RAG，检索召回率提升了 47-65%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG 为临床检索增强系统提供了一个关键的时间维度，弥补了现有知识图谱在纵向临床推理中的不足，显著提升了 LLM 处理时间相关医疗问题的性能。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [56] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了预训练语料排序对模型时序知识获取的影响，并通过对比实验证明，按时间顺序进行预训练能显著改善大模型对事实知识的时效性感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型多使用随机混洗的语料进行预训练，导致模型知识“冻结”，且模型对事实与其对应时间区间的关联能力较差，缺乏对知识时效性的理解。

Method: 1. 构建基准：创建了包含7000多个时序事实性问题及对应评估协议的测试集。2. 实验对比：预训练多个6B参数模型，对比“按时间顺序排列的语料库”与“传统混洗（shuffled）语料库”的训练效果。

Result: 1. 顺序训练的模型在通用语言理解和通用知识上与传统模型持平。2. 顺序训练模型展现出更强的事实新鲜度（factual freshness）和时序精确度。3. 混洗训练模型由于重复次数较多，更倾向于记忆过时的历史数据。

Conclusion: 按时间顺序进行预训练（Temporally ordered pre-training）能够显著提升LLM对事物的时效性认知与精确度，且不会牺牲通用语言能力。该研究为LLM的持续学习与时序知识更新提供了新的范式和基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [57] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中存在的“隐性政治偏见”，定义了两项衡量偏见的指标，并提出了一种名为“政治一致性训练（PCT）”的强化学习方法，成功在不降低模型性能的前提下减轻了这种偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLMs）在处理对立政治话题时存在不对称的偏见，即“隐性政治偏见”，需对其进行量化评估并加以缓解。

Method: 1. 提出两种衡量隐性偏见的指标：情感一致性（Sentiment Consistency）和帮助性一致性（Helpfulness Consistency）；2. 开发了政治一致性训练（PCT），这是一种结合情感一致性训练与帮助性一致性训练的强化学习框架。

Result: 研究发现LLMs在处理对立政治议题时确实存在系统性不对称，通过PCT方法可以显著降低这种隐性政治偏见，且不会损害模型的整体帮助性。

Conclusion: 提出了政治一致性训练（PCT），该方法能有效降低LLM的隐性政治偏见，同时保持模型表现，并在未训练的基准测试中展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对六款主流AI聊天机器人的评估显示，尽管其在英语事实问答中表现极佳，但面临严峻的地区性偏见（偏爱英语语料）、依赖检索质量以及对错误前提缺乏防御能力等系统性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人已成为获取新闻的重要渠道，但此前缺乏针对这些系统在不同语言和区域处理实时突发新闻事实的准确性、跨地区公平性及检索机制健壮性的系统性评估。

Method: 该研究进行了为期14天（2026年2月9日至22日）的评估，涵盖六款主流AI聊天模型（Gemini系列、Grok 4、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、GPT-5/4o mini）。研究者从六个地区（美加、阿拉伯、阿非利加、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）采集了2100个当日BBC新闻事实问题，分别通过多项选择题和自由回答两种形式对模型进行测试，并分析了模型在面对陷阱问题时的反应。

Result: 1. 最优模型多选题准确率达90%以上，但自由回答准确率下降11-13%；2. 印地语表现最差（79%），存在明显的检索英语偏见；3. 超过70%的错误由检索失败导致，而非推理失败；4. 模型极易被带有错误前提的问题诱导（准确率降至19-70%）；5. 发现了“检测-准确度悖论”，即前提检测能力与正确答案恢复能力之间存在部分独立性。

Conclusion: 当前顶尖AI聊天机器人在事实问答任务中表现优异，但其高准确率掩盖了严重的系统性问题：地区性事实获取的不公平性（偏向英语语料）、对检索准确性的极端依赖，以及对带有误导性前提的问题缺乏稳健的辨别能力。这些局限性揭示了AI模型在处理实时新闻和跨语言信息流时的潜在偏见。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [59] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 ConvexTok 的新分词算法，通过凸优化替代传统的贪婪策略来实现全局最优分词构建，实验表明其能有效降低语言模型的 BpB 并提供性能最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流分词算法（如 BPE 和 Unigram）多基于贪婪策略，缺乏全局视野，无法保证构建出的词表在整体上达到最优。

Method: 将分词构建过程表述为一个线性规划（Linear Programming）问题，并使用凸优化（Convex Optimization）工具进行求解。在此框架下，用户可以计算出当前分词方案距离理论最优值的下界（Lower Bound）。

Result: ConvexTok 在内在分词指标和语言模型的位字节（BpB）上均优于现有的贪婪算法；在下游任务性能上有一定提升但改善不完全一致；且经验证，在常用词表大小下，其性能均在理论最优值的 1% 以内。

Conclusion: ConvexTok 证明了将分词构建转化为线性规划问题的可行性。该算法不仅提供了比贪婪算法（如 BPE/Unigram）更优的性能，还引入了全局最优性验证机制，为分词选择提供了理论上的稳健性。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Throughput-Optimal Multiresource-Job Scheduling with Continuous Requirement Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21715)
*Heyuan Yao,Willow Kowalik,Izzy Grosof*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了首个针对连续资源需求分布的多资源任务（MRJ）吞吐量最优调度策略，通过离散化处理显著提升了处理大规模、高多样性任务请求的系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于类别的MRJ调度模型难以处理实际系统中资源需求分布广泛、离散值极多的情况，导致其在实际应用中表现较差，因此需要针对连续资源需求分布的模型进行研究。

Method: 引入了适用于连续MRJ模型的吞吐量最优调度策略（含抢占式和非抢占式），并结合基于系统负载与资源需求分布的离散化方法，提出了具有更高计算效率的策略族。

Result: 通过参数化分布测试及Google Borg集群调度器的真实轨迹数据验证，证明了该策略族在保持吞吐量最优的同时，在计算效率和性能上均优于既有的索引基（index-based）调度策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的吞吐量最优调度策略族有效解决了连续多资源任务（MRJ）队列模型中的调度难题，在理论上实现了最优性，并在实际负载下表现出优于现有策略的状态级性能。

Abstract: Modern computing systems process jobs with resource requirements such as CPU and memory, which are described by multiresource jobs (MRJ) queueing models. In practice, job resource requirements are spread out over so many values, that it is rare to see the same value twice. This pattern is best modeled by a continuous distribution of requirement values. However, the existing theoretical work on stability or throughput-optimality focuses on queueing models with class-based resource requirements. In class-based models, the number of distinct resource requirements must be small to demonstrate strong empirical performance, making them a poor match for these practical systems.
  We introduce the first throughput-optimal family of scheduling policies for the continuous MRJ model, with both preemptive and nonpreemptive variants. We further introduce several efficient policy families, which remain throughput-optimal while considerably improving computational efficiency, under some distributional assumptions. We use a discretization approach, where we choose the discretization granularity based on the system load and the distribution of resource requirements. We validate the real-world applicability of our policies by comparing them against existing index-based policies on parametrized distributions and on datacenter trace data from the Google Borg scheduler, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>

### [61] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了MOOD基准以应对大模型的分布外（OOD）对齐失败，研究发现集成OOD检测器可显著提升安全审计性能，效果优于单纯增加模型参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的安全性与对齐失败往往源于分布外（OOD）情境，即开发者未预见到的异常提示或响应模式；现有安全卫士模型难以泛化至这些OOD场景。

Method: 构建了MOOD（Misalignment Out Of Distribution）基准测试，通过限制训练集分布并评估多种OOD场景；实验测试了四种OOD检测器，并将Mahalanobis距离与困惑度（Perplexity）检测器集成至安全卫士模型中。

Result: 现有的安全卫士模型在处理OOD对齐失败时泛化能力较差；将Mahalanobis距离和基于困惑度的OOD检测与安全卫士模型结合，可将检测召回率从39%提升至45%；此外，该复合架构表现出正向的正比缩放趋势（Scaling Trends）。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为大型语言模型监控系统的核心组成部分；相较于单纯扩大安全卫士模型（Guard Model）的参数规模，将OOD检测与安全卫士结合能实现更显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [62] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0原型，通过更新数据模型和增强AI就绪性，旨在解决现有AOP-Wiki架构局限，从而更好地支持自动化/定量AOP研究及下一代化学品风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AOP-Wiki数据模型和基础设施存在局限，制约了AOP的持续扩展，无法满足计算毒理学、定量AOP和监管科学日益增长的需求；同时引入Agentic AI以促进数据现代化。

Method: 基于证据模型原型（EMOD 3.0），通过扩展数据模型，强化数据结构化以提升AOP的FAIR（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可重用）性和AI就绪性，并改进AOP框架与新方法（NAMs）的整合。

Result: 提出了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，实现了数据模型的扩展，支持更复杂的生物学尺度模拟，优化了内部质量控制，增强了系统对自动化生成AOP和定量建模的支撑能力。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0为AOP知识库的现代化转型提供了新框架，旨在通过优化数据模型提升其对计算驱动的AOP、定量AOP（qAOP）以及下一代风险评估的支持能力。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [63] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算手段对1600多份大屠杀幸存者证词进行了对比分析，证实了学界对于南加州大学和耶鲁大学档案库访谈风格差异的认知，但同时指出这种“结构化vs自由式”的二分法存在明显局限，并提出了一套可推广的数字口述历史分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域一直认为南加州大学大屠杀基金会（结构化、访谈员引导）和耶鲁大学Fortunoff视频档案（自由、开放式）的访谈风格迥异，本研究旨在通过实证分析来检验这一长期存在的学术共识。

Method: 使用了大规模计算分析方法，处理了超过1600份访谈录音，具体手段包括：话语分割（discourse segmentation）、主题建模（topic modeling）以及基于大型语言模型（LLM）的分析，用以量化访谈的“结构化程度”。

Result: 研究结果证实了上述两个档案库在结构上确实存在差异，但也揭示了两者在单个访谈内部及共同叙事模式上存在显著重叠，纠正了过去过于简单化的分类观点。

Conclusion: 该研究挑战了口述历史中“结构化与自由式”的二分法假设，证明了两者之间存在显著的重叠和复杂性。此外，该研究提供了一套可扩展且可重复的计算分析框架，为数字人文和叙事分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [64] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom 是一种通过分解推理逻辑并组合“思维模式”来合成高质量、可控难度推理数据的框架，旨在提升大模型的深度逻辑推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 推理数据合成方法缺乏对导致问题难度等结构性因素的深入理解，导致生成的数据多样性不足且难以有效控制难度，限制了 frontier-level（前沿水平）推理能力的提升。

Method: 提出 MindLoom 框架：1. 将复杂推理分解为“思维模式链”；2. 训练检索模型以匹配问题状态与思维模式；3. 通过迭代应用思维模式构建新问题（组合式工程）；4. 采用基于 Rollout（展开）的判别机制对生成问题进行难度与正确性评估。

Result: 在 5 个 STEM 学科和 4 个数学推理任务的 9 个基准测试中，使用 MindLoom 数据进行微调的模型表现优于基准模型、蒸馏方案及其他外部数据方法；消融实验证实了各核心组件的有效性。

Conclusion: MindLoom 通过将推理过程分解为“思维模式（thought modes）”，实现了对推理数据生成过程的精细化控制。实验结果表明，该方法能够生成高质量、高难度且多样化的 STEM 与数学推理数据，显著提升模型的逻辑推理能力，且优于现有的蒸馏和外部数据基准。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [65] [Evaluation of Pipelines for Data Integration into Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22304)
*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 KGI-Bench 的基准测试框架，通过三个质量指标评估知识图谱整合流水线的性能，并提供相关数据集，旨在帮助开发者从多种流水线方案中选择最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏评估知识图谱（KG）整合流水线整体质量和性能的通用方法，导致在处理复杂数据整合任务时难以确定最佳构建方案。

Method: 提出 KGI-Bench 基准测试框架，采用覆盖度 (coverage)、正确性 (correctness) 和一致性 (consistency) 三个互补的质量指标来评估知识图谱更新后的结果；并构建了包含种子KG、多格式输入数据及参考KG（基准真值）的电影领域数据集。

Result: 通过 KGI-Bench 对12条不同的整合流水线进行了对比实验，量化分析了流水线在不同数据格式和设计选择下的表现，验证了该评估方法的有效性。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench 为评估知识图谱整合流水线提供了一个标准化的框架。通过对12条流水线的实证分析，研究表明该基准能有效识别不同配置下的性能差异，为选择最优整合策略提供了依据。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [66] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过测试AI模型在非典型和错误范畴下的分类能力，发现其对日常概念的理解与人类存在严重的“概念错位”，这种认知偏差会直接转化为AI安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现不仅智能、而且具备像人类一样对日常概念有合理理解的AI系统（即为了安全和可解释性），研究者试图超越传统评估方法，通过探测模型的“概念边界”来揭露模型在常识理解上的缺陷。

Method: 该研究采用心理学经典实验（Rosch and Mervis）作为基础，系统地对比了AI模型与人类在“典型范畴成员”（如：车是交通工具吗？）与“非典型/不合理范畴成员”（如：橄榄是交通工具吗？）分类任务上的表现。此外，研究还评估了模型将对象分配到错误范畴（跨范畴分配）的能力，并分析了这些概念错位对下游应用任务的影响。

Result: 研究发现AI模型在概念边界理解上与人类存在显著差异，例如模型错误地将“词语”归类为“交通工具”或“服装”，将蔬菜归类为水果，甚至将非武器对象归类为“武器”。实验证实了这些概念层面的理解偏差会导致模型在具体下游任务中表现出不可控且潜在危险的行为。

Conclusion: AI模型在概念理解上与人类存在显著的“概念错位”，这种错位不仅仅是简单的错误，还会导致潜在的AI安全风险。研究表明，仅依靠模型对典型样本的处理能力不足以评估其概念边界，通过非典型样本探测对系统评估至关重要。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [67] [Search-E1: Self-Distillation Drives Self-Evolution in Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22511)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Xuxin Zhang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Search-E1，一种无需复杂架构增量、仅利用 GRPO 和离线自蒸馏即可实现 SOTA 推理能力的自演化框架，证明了简洁训练方案在搜索增强推理中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前搜索增强推理模型依赖于繁琐的外部监督、复杂的辅助模块（如过程奖励模型、树搜索算法或手动设计的奖励塑造），这导致架构臃肿且难以迁移。研究者质疑这些复杂机制是否必要。

Method: 提出 Search-E1 方法：在基础 GRPO 训练框架内，交替执行离线自蒸馏（Offline Self-Distillation, OFSD）。通过让模型在含有“特权上下文”（揭示更高效路径）的条件下进行滚存推理，并利用 Token 级的前向 KL 散度进行对齐，从而在不增加复杂辅助模块的情况下提供密集的每步监督。

Result: 在七项问答（QA）基准测试中，基于 Qwen2.5-3B 的 Search-E1 模型取得了 0.440 的平均精确匹配度（EM），超越了同规模及更大规模的所有开源基线模型。

Conclusion: Search-E1 证明了通过简单的自演化流程（GRPO + OFSD）即可实现高性能的搜索增强推理，推翻了“强化推理过程必须依赖复杂辅助模块”的假设，实现了性能与简洁性的最优平衡。

Abstract: Post-training has become the dominant recipe for turning a language model into a competent search-augmented reasoning agent. A line of recent work pushes its performance further by adding elaborate machinery on top of this standard pipeline. These augmentations import external supervision from stronger external systems, attach auxiliary modules such as process reward models or retrospective critics, restructure the rollout itself with tree search or multi-stage curricula, or shape the reward with hand-crafted bonuses and penalties. Each addition delivers a measurable gain, but each also inflates the training pipeline and ties the recipe to resources or designs that may not always be available. We take a step back and ask whether any of this machinery is actually necessary, and propose Search-E1, a self-evolution method that lets a search-augmented agent improve through only vanilla GRPO interleaved with offline self-distillation (OFSD). After each GRPO round, the policy rolls out on its own training questions. A token-level forward KL objective then aligns the policy's inference-time distribution to its own distribution under a privileged context that exposes a more efficient sibling trajectory. Despite this simplicity, the procedure naturally provides dense per-step supervision. On seven QA benchmarks, Search-E1 reaches $0.440$ average EM with Qwen2.5-3B, surpassing all open-source baselines at both scales. Code and complete version will be made public soon.

</details>


### [68] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现AI对技能习得存在双重影响：过度使用或使用低信息量AI会削弱个人学习；而合理使用AI可以作为互补工具，因此需规范AI的使用模式以促进能力发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在问题解决中日益普及，但其对个人技能发展带来的长期影响尚不明确，尤其是AI是否会阻碍人类自身逻辑推理能力的提升。

Method: 通过受控逻辑推理任务进行实验，分析参与者在获取按需AI辅助情况下的AI使用量与信息量（Informativeness）对个人技能习得的影响。

Result: 结果显示，AI使用量与技能发展呈负相关：重度使用者表现逊于同类 peer，轻度使用者与不使用AI者表现相当。此外，低信息量AI对技能学习具有负面效应，而高信息量AI对短期表现有益，但在提升独立推理能力方面存在异质性影响。

Conclusion: AI对人类技能发展的影响取决于其使用程度和信息量：过度依赖AI会削弱自身技能习得；低信息量AI既不能提高即时表现也无法在移除后维持表现；高信息量AI虽能短期提高效率，但效果因人而异。因此，需通过监管AI使用方式来平衡其作为“助手”或“替代品”的双重角色。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [69] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将语言模型的拒绝抑制解释为一种潜在空间规避攻击，证明传统消融方法仅触及决策边界；据此提出的“受控潜在空间规避”（CLE）方法通过越过边界进一步推动模型表征，实现了更强的越狱效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拒绝抑制方法（如激活消融）虽然有效，但缺乏原理性的解释，且仅能将模型表示移动至决策边界，抑制效果有限。研究旨在从潜在空间动力学层面对攻击进行理论分析并优化性能。

Method: 1. 将拒绝抑制建模为针对线性探测器的“潜在空间规避攻击”；2. 指出传统消融方法仅将表示投影到决策边界（最低置信度）；3. 提出“受控潜在空间规避”（Controlled Latent-space Evasion, CLE）算法，通过优化投影参数，将模型表示推至决策边界之外更深处的回答区域。

Result: 该方法在15个指令微调、多模态及推理模型上均取得了SOTA（最先进）的攻击成功率，显著优于传统的消融基准模型及专门的越狱攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将拒绝抑制重新定义为潜在空间规避攻击，成功解释了现有消融技术的局限性。所提出的“受控潜在空间规避”（CLE）方法通过将模型表示推入决策边界之外的更高置信度区域，有效提升了破解成功率。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [70] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench 是一个基于真实多轮人机对话的全新情绪智力评估基准，研究发现 LLM 的情绪智力由多种可分离的能力组成，且相比于简单的情绪识别，预测用户偏好对评估模型表现更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪智力（EI）评估方法过度依赖合成提示词、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法真实反映 LLM 在持续、真实的人机交互过程中理解并响应用户情感的能力。

Method: 提出了 AttuneBench 基准，包含 200 个真实的人机多轮对话，通过人工标注（逐轮情感状态、模型行为、偏好响应）来评估 LLM 的情绪智力。评估了 11 种主流 LLM 在情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量方面的表现。

Result: 评估显示，情绪智力的各项能力（如识别与偏好预测）在不同模型间表现出很大独立性；偏好对齐和响应质量评价对模型能力的区分度远高于单纯的情绪标签准确率。

Conclusion: 评估情绪智力不能依赖单一聚合指标，应将情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量视为可分离的能力。AttuneBench 为诊断LLM在真实多轮对话中的情绪交互能力提供了重要框架。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [71] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用AI平台用户日志衡量职业暴露存在严重偏差，因为数据反映的是平台用户特征而非整体劳动力。修正这一偏差后，AI对就业影响的估计值大幅下降，且现有模型严重低估了AI的替代效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛利用AI平台对话日志来衡量职业对AI的暴露程度，但该研究质疑这些数据是否准确反映了整体劳动力市场的特征，而非仅仅反映了特定平台的用户属性。

Method: 通过对比不同AI平台的输入数据、采用劳工统计局（BLS）劳动力份额进行重加权，以及推导就业弹性的概率极限与偏识别边界，定量评估测量误差对职业暴露度量及就业系数的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1. 平台选择对估计结果有巨大影响（因不同平台输入导致系数差异达1.9倍）；2. 对职业数据进行重加权后，估计结果缩减了42%-93%；3. 现有的测量误差导致对“替代效应”的程度估计不足。

Conclusion: 使用人工智能平台日志构建的职业暴露度量指标存在严重的非经典测量误差。该研究建议在量化AI对就业的影响时，必须考虑到平台用户基础的偏差，仅依赖此类数据会导致对AI替代效应的严重误估。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [72] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合因果发现与双极论证框架（BAF）的新型XAI方法，通过构建特征间的支持与对抗逻辑，为机器学习模型的决策过程提供了更具因果性和解释力的逻辑推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI（XAI）方法主要聚焦于识别特征的相关性，但在解释模型“为什么”做出特定决策（深层逻辑与因果机制）方面能力不足。

Method: 1. 利用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系；2. 将因果关系转化为双极论证框架（BAF），用以表示特征间的支持与反对关系；3. 应用半稳定语义（semi-stable semantics）计算各特征的论证扩展，从而解释预测结果。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够有效地对模型预测进行论证式解释，并在解释的深度和逻辑性上优于标准的传统事后解释方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的结合因果推理与双极论证框架的方法，能够提供比传统事后解释方法更深层、更具逻辑性的“为什么”层面的解释，增强了AI系统的透明度。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [73] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过文献调研构建了AI谄媚行为的分类法，并揭示了学界对谄媚定义的认知分歧，旨在为解决AI系统迎合用户导致的偏差问题提供统一的评价框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术界、工业界和政策制定者对“AI谄媚”缺乏统一的定义与度量标准，导致评估不一致、缓解策略失效，且难以有效应对不同形式的谄媚行为。

Method: 1. 系统性文献综述：回顾了70篇AI谄媚相关论文，构建了区分行为对象（立场观点与人格特质）及表现形式（显性与隐性）的分类法；2. 专家调查：对106名AI领域专家进行问卷调查，分析其对谄媚行为认知的共识与分歧。

Result: 1. 现有研究过度集中于对用户信仰的显性谄媚，而对隐性（如框架效应、省略、语气）及针对用户个人特质的谄媚关注不足；2. 专家一致认为谄媚是主要问题（94.3%），但在具体判定标准上存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚（sycophancy）是一个涵盖多种行为的复杂概念，其定义模糊导致研究难以统一。本文提出的分类法和专家调查明确了研究空白，为后续评估、干预及治理提供了共享的术语框架。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied.
