Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [110] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在ASP中引入了参数化模块逻辑程序，不仅增强了程序的模块化结构与实例化能力，还为其与传统ASP提供了统一的语义基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在一阶回答集程序设计（ASP）中缺乏有效模块化机制的问题，以支持更复杂的程序结构化和子程序实例化。

Method: 引入“参数化模块逻辑程序”（parametric modular logic programs）形式化方法，支持带参数和内涵性声明的子程序定义，并验证了其与现有clingo集体控制功能的语义一致性。

Result: 构建了模块化ASP的理论基础，证明了该方法能够捕获clingo中集体控制功能的语义，并实现了模块化与传统ASP形式的有机连接。

Conclusion: 本文通过参数化模块化逻辑程序，提出了一种用于一阶ASP的模块化框架，成功弥合了模块化系统与传统非模块化ASP之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [111] [Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22720)
*Andrii Kryshtal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明主流AI模型在冲突敏感议题上存在显著的安全风险，特别是在处理已定性暴行时易输出虚假平衡观点。研究构建了专门的评估框架以应对这一缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前部署于冲突地区的AI模型缺乏有效的安全评估标准，其输出可能导致虚假信息传播、加剧社会分裂，甚至恶化冲突局势。

Method: 构建了包含90个多轮对话场景的评估框架，测试了来自4个厂商（OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI）的9种模型配置；特别测试了模型在面对已定性冲突事件时表现出的“虚假平衡”倾向及对仇恨言论的识别能力。

Result: 模型故障率在6%至47%之间波动；当用户要求对已有国际法庭定性的极端暴行事件保持“平衡”观点时，5种模型配置的失败率高达80%-100%。

Conclusion: AI模型的冲突敏感性至关重要；模型在处理已定性冲突（如种族灭绝）时表现出极高的误导性（倾向于“虚假平衡”），因此模型选择直接影响相关地区的安全与稳定。作者建议将该评估框架纳入AI对齐评估体系。

Abstract: AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.

</details>


### [112] [Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759)
*Girish Narayanswamy,Maxwell A. Xu,A. Ali Heydari,Samy Abdel-Ghaffar,Marius Guerard,Kara Vaillancourt,Zhihan Zhang,Jake Garrison,Levi Albuquerque,Dimitris Spathis,Hong Yu,Hamid Palangi,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,David G. T. Barrett,Joseph Breda,Jed McGiffin,Yubin Kim,Yuwei Zhang,Naghmeh Rezaei,Samuel Solomon,Karan Ahuja,Tim Althoff,Jake Sunshine,Ming-Zher Poh,Benjamin Yetton,Ari Winbush,Nicholas B. Allen,James M. Rehg,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Yun Liu,John Hernandez,Anupam Pathak,Conor Heneghan,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally,Pushmeet Kohli,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于万亿分钟可穿戴传感器数据预训练的健康基础模型，有效解决了标注数据匮乏及个体异质性问题。通过配合LLM智能体自动优化策略，该模型在多项健康任务中表现优异，并为构建临床验证的个人健康助手铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决可穿戴传感器数据在转化为个性化健康洞察时面临的三大挑战：1. 个体生理与生活方式的异质性导致的特征提取困难；2. 标注数据稀缺且昂贵；3. 如何有效利用海量未标注数据实现高效的任务适应。

Method: 1. 预训练：利用包含500万参与者、超过一万亿分钟的无标签可穿戴传感器数据训练基础模型。
2. 任务评估：在心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康等35项任务上评估模型性能。
3. 自动化任务构建：部署LLM智能体集群自主搜索下游预测头（predictive heads）。
4. 系统落地：将下游预测器集成至个人健康智能体，并通过临床医师评估验证其安全性与相关性。

Result: 模型在35项多样化健康任务中表现优异；验证了模型规模与数据量提升带来的性能协同增益；证明了该表示学习方案支持高效Few-shot学习及稳健的指标估计；通过LLM智能体自动优化显著提升了下游任务性能，且该提升随LLM能力增强而扩大。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过大规模（万亿分钟）传感器数据预训练的健康基础模型，不仅能在多种健康预测任务中展现出卓越的少样本学习和生成能力，还能通过LLM智能体自动优化下游任务性能，为构建上下文感知、更安全且个性化的个人健康智能体提供了坚实的基础。

Abstract: While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

</details>


### [113] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了使用LLM生成Lean形式化证明来解决数学开放问题的能力，并成功证明了其解决部分Erdős问题和OEIS猜想的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数学推理上表现优异，但其不可靠性限制了在数学研究中的实际应用。由于形式化语言（如Lean）能够提供严谨的验证，因此作者试图通过将LLM与形式化证明相结合来解决这一可靠性问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）生成形式化证明语言（Lean）代码，并结合Lean验证器进行自动验证的智能体（agent）设计方案。并在Erdős公开问题和OEIS猜想上进行了大规模实验评估。

Result: 该系统在353个公开的Erdős问题中自主解决了9个；在492个OEIS猜想中证明了44个。目前该技术已应用于多个数学领域。实验对比发现，融合生成与验证机制的智能体虽然在极难问题上成本较高，但在准确性与可靠性上表现优异。

Conclusion: AI辅助形式化证明搜索已展现出解决数学研究问题的巨大潜力；通过LLM与Lean验证器的协同，可以有效地引导AI探索复杂数学难题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [114] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过引入通道级独立的擦除与写入门，Gated DeltaNet-2 优化了线性注意力模型的内存更新逻辑，提升了在长上下文和复杂检索任务中的模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力模型（如 Gated DeltaNet 和 KDA）在通过 Delta 规则更新固定大小的循环状态时，使用单一标量门控同时控制旧内容的擦除和新内容的写入，限制了模型对复杂信息的记忆更新能力。

Method: 提出 Gated DeltaNet-2 模型，引入通道级（channel-wise）的独立擦除门（erase gate）和写入门（write gate），解耦了传统 Delta-rule 模型中单一标量门控擦除与写入的绑定关系；结合通道级衰减机制，并推导了相应的块状（chunkwise）高效并行训练算法。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数规模、100B Token 的训练下，Gated DeltaNet-2 在语言建模、常识推理和长窗口检索任务（RULER 基准）中均超过了 Mamba-2、KDA 等同类竞品。

Conclusion: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过解耦擦除与写入操作，有效解决了现有线性注意力机制在内存更新中的局限性，在处理长序列检索任务及大规模语言模型训练中展现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [115] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有智能体“仅能修改文本/提示词”的自进化局限，MOSS提出了一种源代码级别的自重写系统，通过在生产环境中自动定位缺陷、重构代码并进行容器热更新，实现了比传统方法更强大、更通用的智能体自我修正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主智能体在部署后往往是静态的，现有的“自进化”智能体仅能通过修改文本构件（如Prompt、配置文件）进行演化，无法修复程序架构层面的缺陷（路由、状态不变量等）。

Method: 提出MOSS系统：1. 基于生产环境失败证据的自动审计；2. 确定性的多阶段流水线；3. 将代码修改委托给外部编码智能体；4. 通过瞬态试运行验证；5. 用户许可下的容器在位热更新及自动回滚机制。

Result: 在OpenClaw基准测试中，MOSS在无需人工干预的情况下，将四项任务的平均评分从未经优化的0.25提升至0.61。

Conclusion: 源代码级别的自进化（Self-rewriting）是解决自主智能体系统结构性失效的更通用、更稳健的方法，能够突破仅通过文本可变构件进行演化的瓶颈。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynaFlow 是一个旨在简化 ML 模型设备内并行处理的框架，通过将逻辑模型与物理调度解耦，支持更透明、灵活的并行策略集成，在减少开发负担的同时显著提高了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML 系统中设备内并行（intra-device parallelism）虽然能提升资源利用率，但现有的框架执行模型静态且序列化，导致集成并行策略需要繁琐且不可移植的人工代码重构，开发成本极高，难以适应多变的硬件与模型环境。

Method: 提出了一种新型架构方案：1. 前端（Frontend）：支持图划分标注，提供可编程接口以定义自定义并行策略；2. 后端（Backend）：处理异步控制/数据流，引入自定义内存管理消除数据拷贝开销，并兼容现有 CUDA Graphs 和 TorchInductor 优化。

Result: DynaFlow 在 6 个先进的 ML 系统中实现了并行策略的透明集成，仅需极少量代码修改即可达到最高 1.29 倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynaFlow 解决了个性化设备内并行策略开发复杂的问题，通过解耦逻辑定义与物理调度，降低了工程成本并实现了高性能，为 ML 系统提供了透明、灵活的并行化方案。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [117] [LiveR: Fine-Grained Elasticity via Live Reconfiguration for Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22014)
*Haoyuan Liu,Kairui Zhou,Shuyao Qi,Qinwei Yang,Shengkai Lin,Shizhen Zhao,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LiveR 是一种用于大模型训练的实时重配置运行时，通过异步内存切换机制替代传统的存盘重启，将模型训练的弹性调整开销从分钟级降低至秒级，实现了高效利用廉价 GPU 资源进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了利用廉价但易被回收的 GPU 资源（如 Spot 实例）进行大模型训练，必须缩短因资源变动导致的训练停机时间，而现有的“先存盘、后重启”的弹性机制开销过大。

Method: 提出了一种实时重配置运行时 LiveR，核心技术包括：异步准备目标环境以规避初始化延迟、跨多维度（张量、流水线、数据并行）的在线模型状态流式传输与重塑，以及轻量级的状态切换提交机制。它取代了传统的基于存储的停启式（checkpoint-and-restart）重配置逻辑。

Result: 在 Megatron-LM 和 PyTorch 上的实验表明：相比传统的 Checkpoint/Restart，重配置速度提升了 14-23 倍；将停机时间从分钟级压缩至秒级；在波动资源环境下维持了高达 99% 的训练吞吐效率，且几乎无稳态性能损耗。

Conclusion: LiveR 通过将昂贵的重启和存储检查点过程转化为在线的内存处理，有效地解决了大模型弹性训练中的停机问题，显著提高了 volatile 计算资源在 LLM 训练中的实用性。

Abstract: To reduce user costs and maximize cluster utilization, large model training increasingly leverages volatile but inexpensive GPU capacity, such as spot instances and reclaimable resources in shared clusters. Yet, capitalizing on these economic benefits requires jobs to adapt within the short warning windows that many such environments provide. Existing elastic training systems still treat reconfiguration as stop-and-restart: they externalize distributed state through checkpoints, rebuild the distributed runtime on a new topology, and restart training, turning each resize event into a storage-heavy recovery procedure that incurs substantial downtime from checkpoint I/O, process restart, CUDA initialization, and communicator setup. We present LiveR, a live reconfiguration runtime for elastic LLM training that replaces storage-backed restart with a live, bounded-memory handoff between mixed-parallel training worlds. While the current world continues training, LiveR asynchronously prepares the target world, bootstraps newly added workers in isolation to keep heavyweight initialization off the critical path, and streams model state directly over high-bandwidth interconnects while reshaping it online across tensor, pipeline, and data parallel dimensions. Once the target world is ready, LiveR performs a lightweight commit that switches training to the new configuration without stop-and-restart on the live path. We implement LiveR atop Megatron-LM and PyTorch and evaluate it end-to-end on a multi-node GPU cluster. Across diverse reconfiguration scenarios, LiveR reduces downtime from minutes to seconds, accelerates reconfiguration by 14$\times$-23$\times$ over checkpoint/restart baselines, incurs minimal steady-state overhead, and sustains up to 99% training goodput under volatile-resource conditions, making volatile low-cost GPU capacity far more practical for LLM training.

</details>


### [118] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SPDC 框架通过一种轻量级加密与并行计算组合方案，在保护数据隐私的同时，实现了在边缘服务器上的快速、安全、可验证的矩阵行列式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算与 IoT 环境中，矩阵行列式计算 (MDC) 因传统算法立方级复杂度而难以满足实时需求，同时在不可信的边缘服务器上进行计算时，必须解决数据隐私与计算结果完整性保障问题。

Method: 1. 隐私保护：引入“复合元素失真 (CED)”技术，结合“元素级混淆 (EWO)”和“Panth 旋转定理 (PRT)”进行加密；
2. 计算优化：采用并行 LU 分解将加密矩阵分布到多个不信任的边缘服务器，并采用单向通信模型消除服务器间交互；
3. 验证机制：提出两种验证算法（概率标量法 Q_2 和确定性低复杂度法 Q_3）以确保结果完整性。

Result: 数学分析表明该框架提供了强大的安全与隐私保障，在降低计算开销的同时实现了高效、可扩展且灵活的部署，有效支持边缘场景下的实时 MDC。

Conclusion: SPDC 框架通过创新的加密和并行计算方案，成功在不安全的边缘服务器环境中实现了高效、安全且隐私保护的行列式计算 (MDC)，非常适合资源受限的 IoT 场景。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [119] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Nf-PEAK，一套能在受限和包含干扰的 Kubernetes 环境下，通过非线性模型将节点级功耗（RAPL）精确分解并归因至单个 Nextflow 任务的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在 Kubernetes 集群中执行时，任务间异构性巨大。由于 Intel RAPL 仅提供节点级能耗、权限限制以及并发负载带来的资源竞争与噪声，目前在共享集群上精确测量单个任务的能耗极其困难。

Method: 提出了一种容器化方法 Nf-PEAK：(1) 识别工作流 Pod；(2) 通过 cgroup 元数据将 Pod 映射到宿主机进程；(3) 采样 RAPL 和每进程性能计数器；(4) 应用非线性能量信用模型，最终将能耗聚合到任务级别。

Result: 在 Kubernetes 实验中，Nf-PEAK 在隔离运行时的平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）为 6.6%，在存在其他工作负载干扰时为 10.9%，且在多节点扩缩容下保持稳定，其准确性优于同类工具 Kepler。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK 是一种在 Kubernetes 上评估工作流任务级能耗的有效工具，在隔离及受干扰环境下均表现出比现有技术（如 Kepler）更高的准确性，能有效应对资源争用与噪声，为科学工作流的能耗优化提供了可靠数据支持。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [120] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对云Spot实例价格波动和中断风险，通过AI预测模型实现跨区域集群的成本感知自动部署，在保持高预测准确率的同时显著降低了云计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云服务（如AWS EC2 Spot）无法在部署前评估集群成本，且限制单区域部署，缺乏对动态定价和供应中断风险的预判能力，难以满足弹性基础设施对高经济效益的需求。

Method: 提出了一种AI驱动的供应服务，将云资源供应计划监控与预测模型相结合，实现部署前的配置预测与定价估算，并支持跨区域的成本感知决策。

Result: 系统支持高达1500 vCPU的集群规模，测试结果显示与AWS EC2 Spot服务的预测准确率高达99.79%，且通过跨区域调度可实现最高64%的潜在成本节约。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的AI驱动型多区域Spot实例配置服务有效提升了云资源分配的预测性与经济性，能够通过利用区域间定价差异显著降低成本，同时保持与现有云服务行为的一致性。

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需因果充分性与预处理假设的局部因果协变量选择方法，通过局部边界搜索显著提升了高维因果效应估计的计算效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局因果结构学习（计算开销大），或依赖不切实际的假设（如因果充分性假设、预处理假设），在处理高维数据时存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种局部学习方法：通过刻画一个包含至少一个有效调节集（adjustment set）的“局部边界”（local boundary），并在该边界内进行高效的局部识别搜索。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持高精度因果效应估计的同时，显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的、计算高效的局部学习方法，在无需预处理变量假设或因果充分性假设的情况下，能够有效进行因果效应估计，且具有理论上的完备性和正确性。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [122] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对离线广告拍卖保留价评估的风险，本文提出了一种支持感知的决策框架，通过统计验证替代单点评估，有效过滤掉高估或有风险的策略，确保证策上线前的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告拍卖中，仅依赖重放表（replay tables）进行离线预估存在显著风险，容易掩盖弱支撑、多重比较偏差、子群损害及投标响应不确定性。现有方法仅关注数值估计或排名，缺乏对证据效力是否足以支持策略落地的决策支撑。

Method: 提出了一种支持感知（support-aware）的离线决策框架。该框架将历史日志数据转化为由“已认证策略”、“被统计支配的替代方案”以及“需进一步验证的候选者”组成的保守决策对象；定义了统一的有限目录保证（finite-catalog guarantee），并结合了局部重放泛化理论、信息论阈值分辨率限制及异构投标响应分析。

Result: 在iPinYou数据集上的实验显示，该框架将19个策略的目录缩减为2个策略的验证短名单，并在44个细分市场中证明了无损害性。其主要的保留规则在实测中实现了显著的收益提升（如第三季冻结数据下43.87%的提升），且提升指标具有稳健的统计下界。

Conclusion: 离线保留价拍卖策略评估不应仅依赖单点估计排名，而应转向基于“验证集”（validation decisions）的框架。通过结合统计不确定性控制与保守支撑门槛，该框架能在保证评估质量的同时，有效过滤掉不可信的策略，从而做出更稳健的决策。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [123] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 这是一份2017-2018年编写的技术报告，旨在通过“在线博弈策略的财富保证”这一博弈论方法，重新证明概率论中经典的重对数律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过一种不同于传统统计学或概率论的途径——即利用在线博弈策略的财富增长保证——来探讨和证明重对数律。

Method: 利用博弈论框架下的财富分配策略（即通过投资策略在概率空间中实现财富最大化），结合在线学习理论中的博弈界限来重新推导重对数律。

Result: 该技术报告成功展示了如何将金融博弈中的财富累积与概率论中的经典极限理论（LIL）建立联系，提供了一种基于博弈论视角的重对数律分析方法。

Conclusion: 通过在线博弈策略的财富增长保证，成功推导并证明了重对数律（Law of the Iterated Logarithm, LIL）。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [124] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复的一价拍卖中“托（shilling）”对竞标学习算法的影响。即便托不改变拍卖结果，但通过干扰反馈信息极大增加了学习难度。作者提出了一种自适应的鲁棒学习算法，实现了接近最优的 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在重复的一价拍卖中，即便“托”行为不直接干预分配过程，仅通过操纵反馈信息（如竞标者获悉的是真实竞标与虚假竞标的最大值）如何影响学习者的决策效率与遗憾值上限。

Method: 提出了一种结合两种策略的算法：(1) 鲁棒性区间消除分支（robust interval-elimination branch），在托干扰严重时降低复杂性；(2) 乐观更新分支（optimistic branch），在去偏差后利用后缀信息进行学习。并通过验证与竞速机制（validation and racing procedure）实现了在无需预知尺度或反馈几何结构下的自适应学习。

Result: 算法在处理受干扰反馈时达到了 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 的遗憾界，且在单活跃区域案例下给出了匹配的下界（对数因子除外），证明了该机制在理论上的最优性。

Conclusion: 仅通过反馈干扰的“托（shilling）”行为会显著改变重复拍卖的统计学习难度，虽然托在分配过程中不直接影响胜负，但其对观测数据的干扰使得学习算法面临复杂的探索与利用权衡。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [125] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对 HSIC 类独立性检验中计算昂贵的置换校准问题，本文提出了基于鞅的 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC 统计量，无需重采样即可实现渐近正态分布，在保持检验性能的情况下计算速度提升了数十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 HSIC 和 dHSIC 统计量属于退化的 V-统计量，其零分布依赖于数据且无法简单推导，实践中必须依赖于昂贵的置换检验（permutation calibration），导致计算开销过大（通常增加两个数量级）。

Method: 采用基于鞅（martingale）的 MMD 构造方法。mHSIC 构造了一个自归一化的下三角 Hadamard 乘积和，以在全样本下实现渐近正态性；mdHSIC 则采用半样本拆分（half-sample split）策略，在处理多变量联合独立性时，将条件均值残差抑制到指数级衰减，从而在任意固定维度下均保持渐近标准正态分布。

Result: 在 1 到 500 的输入维度及 2 到 10 个联合变量的实验设定下，mHSIC 和 mdHSIC 与置换校准的基准相比，表现出相似的 I 类错误率和统计功效，同时计算速度提升了 25 到 60 倍。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC 统计量通过鞅（martingale）方法彻底消除了对重采样（permutation）校准的需求，实现了在保持统计功效的同时，显著降低了计算成本，为高维独立性检验提供了高效、稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [126] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种通过核密度估计实现“保守漂移”的一步生成模型算法，通过确保速度场为梯度场解决了非保守问题，并证明了其在有限采样情况下的明确收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决通用基于位移的漂移场在一步生成模型中存在的非保守问题（即速度场非梯度场问题），并为有限样本条件下的性能提供理论收敛保证。

Method: 提出一种“保守漂移方法”，用基于KDE的梯度速度取代原有的基于位移的漂移速度；利用联合熵恒等式推导有限粒子收敛界；引入互惠KDE自相互作用项进行误差校正；对非保守漂移方法（拉普拉斯核）进行了对比分析，利用锐核分解速度场。

Result: 证明了保守漂移方法在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上的有限粒子收敛界；得到了显式的收敛速率，在特定正则条件下达到 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$，在更优生长条件下达到 $N^{-(2-\beta)/(2(d+4-\beta))}$；建立了连续时间残差速度界到一步生成保证的映射。

Conclusion: 该研究提出并证明了一种用于一步生成模型的保守漂移方法，该方法通过使用基于核密度估计（KDE）的梯度速度确保了场的可积性和收敛性，并提供了明确的有限粒子收敛速度理论保证。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于VR的共享控制框架，使操作员能够通过直观的沉浸式界面实时引导无人机团队进行复杂环境下的导航，有效弥补了完全自主系统在应对不可预见条件时的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化的多机器人系统在面对非结构化环境、突发状况以及理解操作员意图时存在局限性，需要一种人机协同机制来提升复杂环境下的任务执行能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于VR的共享控制框架，核心包括：1. 运动基元运动规划器（Motion-primitive-based planner），支持在集成操作员输入的同时生成无碰撞轨迹；2. 导纳控制器（Admittance controller），用于动态调整团队行为；3. 双向VR交互接口，支持迁移点引导及实时视觉反馈。系统支持物理与仿真环境的混合现实操作。

Result: 实验表明，该共享控制框架显著改善了障碍物躲避性能，能够有效保持智能体间距，并降低了操作员在控制多机器人团队时的认知负荷。

Conclusion: 基于VR的共享控制框架有效地弥补了纯自动导航在非结构化环境下的不足，证明了人机协同在提升复杂多智能体执行任务效率和鲁棒性方面的可行性与优势。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [128] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对无人机集群，提出了一种无需全局协调的分布式多覆盖算法，解决了在资源受限和部分故障环境下，保障关键目标冗余观测的可靠性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集中式整数规划方法无法应对无人机集群在大规模、受限通信、受限计算及系统部分失效场景下的实际部署需求，需解决多覆盖冗余及动态可靠性问题。

Method: 设计了一种分布式重叠覆盖（multicoverage）算法，仅利用机器人本地感知和本地通信机制，无需全局协调即可完成任务分配与覆盖维护。

Result: 算法在仅依赖本地信息的情况下，实现了对关键目标的冗余观测，并能在应对机器人失效时，保持对关键资产的可靠覆盖。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种无需全局协调的分布式多覆盖算法，能够有效解决无人机集群在受限通信与计算条件下的任务冗余与覆盖鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [129] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于闭环视觉反馈的强化学习方法，成功实现了脱离复杂动力学建模的微纤维形状精准操控，验证了简化仿真模型策略在真实微尺度环境下直接部署的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微尺度下的表面及界面相互作用难以精确建模，导致传统的基于模型控制和仿真训练方法在实际应用中受限，难以实现准确的接触式微操作。

Method: 提出了一种闭环仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的强化学习方法；在理想化的无摩擦仿真环境中训练几何形状控制策略，并将该策略直接部署至双轴夹持微操作平台，通过高频（40 Hz）视觉反馈实时修正模型未考虑到的表面相互作用偏差。

Result: 该策略在无需重训练或域适应的情况下，成功实现了对不同直径（50-120 μm）和长度（10-20 mm）丝绸微纤维的稳定操控，在24种初始构型测试中，平均点对点形状误差仅为 270 ± 80 μm。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当仿真与现实的偏差可以通过实时闭环反馈进行修正时，仅在简化仿真环境中训练的强化学习策略即可实现微尺度物体（如微纤维）的有效操纵，无需繁琐的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移或重训练。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [130] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多肢机器人如何在微重力稀疏锚点环境下实现动态运动，通过提出一种参数化运动规划框架，揭示了扩大接触力空间和抑制冲量动力学对提升运动稳定性和降低驱动成本的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的运动依赖于稀疏且不规则的抓取点，需要通过严格管理肢体接触与机器人全身协调控制来实现动态移动，现有研究缺乏针对此类复杂约束下的系统设计准则。

Method: 提出一个可参数化的运动规划框架，通过调整步态、步长、运动速度和标准姿态等参数，在物理仿真环境中对两种代表性的四足机器人形态进行评估，考核指标包括稳态性能和驱动需求。

Result: 实验结果表明，优化的接触配置与平滑的全身动力学协作能显著提高运动的稳定性和效率，并降低对驱动器的需求。

Conclusion: 增加接触力旋量空间（contact wrench space）的大小并减弱冲量式的全身动力学响应是提升微重力环境下多肢机器人运动性能的关键。这些发现为微重力下多肢系统的接触点选择与全身协调控制策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [131] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG是一个无需额外人工标注的数据生成框架，通过利用单个演示迭代生成物理合法的恢复行为，大幅提升了接触丰富型双臂操作的鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富型双臂操作的行为克隆受限于高昂的数据采集成本，且系统在偏离演示路径时缺乏恢复策略，导致鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出PGDG框架，包含物理驱动的采样器（用于生成物理合法的恢复轨迹）与数据集策展人（用于筛选信息量大、非冗余的样本以优化采样分布），并结合短时域采样控制对风险状态进行重标注（relabeling）。

Result: 在四项双臂操作任务中性能显著提升；以RotateBox-Pitch任务为例，模拟器成功率从38%提升至93%，现实世界从35%提升至82%；在GR00T等基础模型微调中亦有显著增益。

Conclusion: PGDG通过物理驱动的采样与精细筛选机制，有效解决了接触丰富型双臂操作中数据稀缺及偏离流形（off-manifold）的鲁棒性问题，显著提升了行为克隆策略在仿真及零样本现实世界的性能，并能有效赋能大模型微调。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [132] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对未知环境下的多机器人自适应覆盖问题，本文提出了一种基于在线更新环境参数模型的遍历轨迹优化策略，显著提升了覆盖效率与资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遍历搜索方法在处理未知环境先验分布时表现不佳，难以高效地将机器人分配至不断变化的目标采样区域。

Method: 提出了一种自适应覆盖策略，增强了传统的遍历轨迹优化（ergodic trajectory optimization）；通过在线更新环境的参数化模型来构建实时目标空间信息分布，并据此调整机器人的采样行为。

Result: 仿真实验结果表明，该框架能有效提升机器人对高兴趣区域的覆盖效率，优化资源分配，并实现有效的个体控制策略合成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应覆盖策略通过在线更新环境参数模型，有效解决了未知环境下多机器人覆盖效率低和资源分配不均的问题。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [133] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePBDS 提出了一种几何一致的框架，通过回拉控制障碍函数将任务空间的复杂约束转化为配置空间的线性约束，并允许接入高层策略实现安全的灵巧操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要在多个异构几何空间（如配置空间 $\mathbb{R}^7$、末端执行器空间 $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ 等）之间协调复杂的目标与安全性约束，现有的方法难以同时兼顾几何一致性与安全性。

Method: 1. 使用“回拉控制障碍函数”（Pullback Control Barrier Functions）将任务空间的约束映射回配置空间；2. 设计“任务流形动作接口”（Task Manifold Action Interface）允许外部策略注入残差运动，同时保持底层的安全性。

Result: 在23自由度Frank-Panda-Allegro手平台上验证：灵巧抓取成功率达92.5%；3指抓取成功率达94.4%；实现了模型预测控制下的完全驱动手掌朝下在手重定向（超过360°偏航角），且鲁棒性强。

Conclusion: SafePBDS 通过在几何一致的框架内整合任务流形动力学与控制障碍函数，为高维度机器人灵巧操作实现了高效、可确证的安全控制，并在复杂的模拟和真实硬件任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [134] [Analytical and Experimental Force Analysis of a Soft Linear Pneumatic Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21836)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过理论建模与实验验证，阐明了线性软套管致动器（LSSA）在不同压力、位移和负载条件下的力生成特性，揭示了轴向刚度对输出性能的制约作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软套管致动器（SSA）的力产生行为（特别是输出力随伸展的变化、外部负载的影响及轴向刚度的作用）尚缺乏深入的理论解释和定量分析。

Method: 构建了一个关于线性软套管致动器（LSSA）的准静态分析模型，将净轴向力表示为由端盖和折叠壁产生的压力贡献减去轴向刚度引起的阻力；并通过规定伸展实验和静态负载实验进行验证。

Result: 实验显示，在125 kPa压力下，输出力从零位移时的112 N衰减至40 mm位移时的近乎为零；静态负载会延迟力的产生并降低输出力，尤其是在低至中等压力下。模型与实验数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: LSSA的力产生机制是内部压力、几何形状、位移、负载以及轴向刚度共同耦合的结果。该研究建立的准静态模型有效地解释了输出力随伸展量增加而衰减的物理机制。

Abstract: Soft sleeve actuators (SSAs) have recently been developed as a pneumatic actuation approach for wearable and assistive robotic systems. By integrating the actuation structure into a sleeve-like geometry, these actuators can reduce reliance on external attachment layers and transmission mechanisms while maintaining compliance with limb-shaped surfaces. However, the force-generation behavior of SSAs remains insufficiently explained, particularly with respect to the variation of output force during extension, the influence of external loading, and the mechanical role of axial stiffness. This paper presents an analytical and experimental force analysis of a linear soft sleeve actuator (LSSA). A quasi-static analytical model was developed by expressing the net axial force as the pressure-generated contribution from the cap and folded walls, reduced by the force associated with axial stiffness. The model incorporates internal pressure, projected pressure areas, folded wall geometry, axial displacement, and an experimentally fitted axial stiffness relation. Prescribed-extension and static-load experiments were conducted to evaluate the actuator response. At 125 kPa, the generated force decreased from approximately 112 N at zero extension to nearly zero at 40 mm. Static loading delayed measurable force generation and reduced force output, particularly at low and intermediate pressures. The results show that LSSA force generation is governed by coupled effects of pressure, geometry, displacement, loading, and axial stiffness.

</details>


### [135] [EvoScene-VLA: Evolving Scene Beliefs Inside the Action Decoder for Chunked Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21862)
*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoScene-VLA 引入循环场景状态更新机制，通过在动作执行过程中追踪并修正场景表示，提升了机器人策略在应对遮挡与环境动态变化时的几何一致性和控制准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分块 VLA 策略通常仅基于当前视觉观测预测动作，忽略了机器人执行动作过程中产生的遮挡、接触和环境变化，导致在视觉采样间隙出现几何信息缺失，难以保持长期的场景状态一致性。

Method: 提出 EvoScene-VLA 模型，引入循环场景前缀（Recurrent scene prefix），在视觉语言模型（VLM）调用间传递动作更新后的场景状态；采用训练阶段的“场景预测器（Scene Predictor）”和“几何锚点（Geometric Anchor）”进行监督，在推理阶段则移除这些模块以实现轻量化。

Result: 在 31 项 RoboTwin 仿真任务中，固定评估成功率从 87.2% 提升至 89.1%，随机评估从 86.1% 提升至 88.5%；在 Galaxea R1-Lite 真实机器人实验中表现超过所有基线模型。

Conclusion: EvoScene-VLA 通过维护“动作更新的场景先验”，成功解决了分块 VLA 策略在感知更新间隙（视觉盲区）的几何一致性问题，提升了机器人操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需通信的多机器人协作框架，通过结合认知逻辑推理、贝叶斯更新和MPPI控制，使机器人在信息受限下能通过预测队友行为实现高效的隐式协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏通信的环境下，传统依赖实时信息交换的多机器人协调策略失效，机器人需要具备通过高阶推理实现隐式协作与长程规划的能力。

Method: 提出了一种新型动态认知规划框架：1. 利用高阶信念粒子进行推理并结合贝叶斯推断更新世界信念；2. 采用行为树（Behavior Tree）实现基于队友决策预期的动作选择；3. 通过时间感知模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器实现推理与底层运动控制的集成。

Result: 仿真和实物实验证明，该方法相比一阶基准模型，有效降低了任务完成时间，展现出在部分可观测环境下良好的适应性。

Conclusion: 基于认知逻辑的动态规划框架可作为通信受限环境下多机器人系统协作的鲁棒基础，显著提升复杂任务的执行效率。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [137] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用低成本自主无人机通过视觉运动追踪进行结构振动监测的非接触式方法，经验证能有效检测结构损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式结构损伤检测方法在某些场景下难以实施，且商业无人机系统成本高昂。亟需开发一种基于低成本无人机的非接触式视觉监测技术，以实现高效、经济的结构健康监测。

Method: 基于视觉的运动追踪技术；利用自定义低成本无人机在GPS拒绝环境下进行自主对准与振动信号采集；将时域位移数据转换至频域进行分析，并与接触式加速度计及有限元模型进行对比验证。

Result: 所提平台成功捕捉到结构基频及其因损伤产生的频移；无人机平台虽存在一定干扰（误差最高达 5.7%），但仍能可靠地识别损伤，且在显著降低成本的同时达到了与商业系统相当的检测性能。

Conclusion: 低成本自主无人机是结构健康监测的一种实用、灵活且可扩展的方案，特别适用于接触式传感受限的场景，且具备多机协同作业的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>


### [138] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对人形机器人引入 MIF 系统，通过不确定性感知 3DGS 和差异驱动的局部内存更新，实现在动态环境下的高可靠导航与安全操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义映射和场景图系统难以处理人形机器人运动引起的感官失真、动态环境变化以及对操作级几何安全性的严苛要求。

Method: 提出 Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF)，包含三个核心功能场：1. 带有不确定性感知 Appearance Field（抑制步态引起的模糊）；2. 维护拓扑记忆的 Spatial Field；3. 支持交互姿态安全（IPS）的 Geometry Field。引入了差异检测评分机制，仅对局部不一致区域进行更新。

Result: 在 Unitree-G1 人形机器人上的实测表明，在非静态环境中导航重定位成功率从 12% 提升至 94%，同时通过特征蒸馏将语义内存占用降低了 91.4%。

Conclusion: MIF 通过将语义 3DGS、差异触发的内存更新和任务驱动的几何重构集成到闭环中，有效解决了人形机器人在动态环境下的导航与操作难题，显著提升了重定位成功率并降低了内存消耗。

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [139] [A Visitation Grid for Complete Coverage Foraging in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21947)
*Qi Arturo Gonzalez,Yifeng Gao,Li Zhang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人群在任务末期收集零散资源效率低的问题，提出了一种基于服务器维护的网格化随机搜索策略，通过引导机器人访问低频区域减少冗余，显著提升了全量资源采集的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人群觅食算法主要关注任务早期的效率，在处理任务末期仅剩少量分散资源时效率低下，且存在大量冗余访问，完成资源的全量收集耗时过长。

Method: 提出了一种基于网格的随机觅食策略：(1) 将未知搜寻区域划分为网格地图，由轻量级中央服务器维护；(2) 服务器根据机器人报告的位置更新网格访问次数，形成全局勘探密度估计；(3) 机器人基于局部 3x3 邻域网格的访问次数，以概率方式选择访问次数最少的区域进行搜索，从而在保持随机性的同时兼顾覆盖率。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该策略在总采集时间上比传统 CPFA 算法缩短了 33%，且在任务后期的采集效率提升了 48% 以上。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了在资源受限的情况下，通过网格化管理资源采集过程，能够显著提升机器人群在任务末期的采集效率，并缩短总体任务时间，具有良好的稳健性、灵活性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The complete collection of sparse resources in large, unknown environments remains a challenging problem for autonomous robot swarms. Previous studies have shown that a substantial portion of total mission time is consumed during the final stage of collection, where only a small fraction of randomly scattered resources remain. Consequently, many existing swarm foraging algorithms (search and collection) focus on collecting most resources within a limited time window, rather than improving end-stage efficiency for collecting all resources. We propose a grid-based stochastic foraging strategy that explicitly reduces redundant visits and accelerates late-stage collection. The unknown search area is partitioned into a grid map, which is maintained by a lightweight central server. To maintain scalability, both robots and the server operate within limited memory and computational constraints. The server updates the grid-level visitation counts based on robot-reported locations, producing a global estimate of the exploration density. For each new foraging trip, a robot selects its next search area from a local 3 X 3 neighborhood of grids probabilistically with the lowest visitation count, thus biasing exploration toward under-visited regions while maintaining stochasticity. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently outperforms the canonical centrally placed baseline foraging algorithm (CPFA). Compared to CPFA, the proposed method reduces the total collection time by up to 33% and improves collection efficiency by more than 48% during the final stage of the mission. These results indicate that the proposed strategy is robust, flexible, and scalable for near-complete and complete resource collection in robot swarms and can serve as a general enhancement for stochastic swarm foraging methods under limited onboard resources.

</details>


### [140] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于摩擦感知的双臂搬运框架，通过实时估计物体质量与质心，利用二阶锥规划（SOCP）优化接触力，实现了对未知惯性属性物体的稳定搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人搬运未知质量和质心位置的物体时，力矩平衡的控制不当会导致物体滑落、姿态偏移或挤压过度，迫切需要一种自适应的处理框架。

Method: 1. 在线估计：利用测得的接触扳手（Wrench）实时估计物体的质量和质心位置；2. 轨迹优化：包含离线轨迹细化阶段以规避环境碰撞；3. 优化控制：通过二阶锥规划（SOCP）在椭球摩擦极限曲面约束下计算各处的接触力和扭矩，将摩擦可行性作为硬约束并最小化接触力度。

Result: 实验证明该框架在不同质心配置下，能够有效处理未知惯性属性的物体，并保持摩擦接触的稳定性。

Conclusion: 提出的双臂处理框架能够在无需预先获知属性的情况下，通过在线估计与基于SOCP的接触力优化，实现未知惯性属性物体的稳定搬运，且无需对防滑和防挤压进行单独调参。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [141] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA 引入了一种基于语义接触上下文的机器人在线自适应框架，通过 Transformer 从力控数据中实时推断接触状态，显著提升了机器人在力主导装配任务中的 Sim-to-Real 迁移效果与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RMA 方法在力主导的装配任务中对模拟器参数的适应能力有限，且在从仿真（Sim）迁移至现实（Real）时性能显著下降，缺乏鲁棒的在线自适应机制。

Method: 提出 CoRMA（对比机器人运动自适应）框架：1. 使用 6D 语义接触上下文（接触发生、侧向啮合、引导过渡、接触方向、卡滞）替代原始模拟器参数；2. 部署一个基于历史力、本体感觉和动作数据的因果 Transformer 适配器进行在线推理；3. 采用语义回归和力状态对比目标进行训练。

Result: 在 Isaac Lab/Sim 仿真环境及真实 Marvin 机械臂的 PegInsert、GearMesh 和 NutThread 任务中，CoRMA 在存在目标位姿噪声的情况下，相比 FORGE 基准模型展现了更高的真实场景任务成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断（Semantic Contact Inference）是一种有效的机器人装配任务自适应接口，能够实现无需示范、无需特权信息且无需梯度更新的实时策略调整。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习不变符号奖励函数的框架，通过从少量演示中提取行为不变量而非拟合视觉特征，解决了机器人强化学习中奖励函数泛化困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉奖励模型在开放世界机器人操作中往往过度依赖特定像素分布，导致泛化能力差。需要一种能够超越实验室受控环境、适应不同场景并仅需少量演示的通用奖励函数设计方法。

Method: 提出了一个由两部分组成的框架：1. 结构化奖励公式（structural reward formulation），用于编码任务策略和物理约束；2. 混合符号-数值过程（hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure），用于从演示中提取不变量。该方法减少了对视觉特征拟合的依赖，转向任务级属性的发现。

Result: 在8个Meta-World任务和3个Franka操作任务上表现优于基准方法，具有更强的过程对齐和策略评估能力；在现实世界的OOD实验中，证明了该方法对位置、视角和物体变化的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过学习任务层面的行为不变性（behavioral invariants），该框架实现了无需在线交互即可从少量演示中提取鲁棒且可泛化的奖励函数，能够在分布外（OOD）场景下实现零样本泛化。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [143] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVP 提出通过引入“视觉原语”作为 VLM 与动作模型之间的接口，解耦认知感知与精细运动控制，显著提升了机器人操作的性能和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型将指令理解、空间感知和运动控制耦合在单一学习目标中，导致动作专家必须隐式重学预训练 VLM 已具备的感知能力，限制了模型的效率和泛化性。

Method: 提出 AVP（Action with Visual Primitives）架构，将 VLM 与动作专家解耦：VLM 推理下一阶段目标并输出视觉原语标记（Visual-primitive tokens），随后这些标记作为条件输入给基于流匹配（flow-matching）的动作专家，并结合末端执行器运动学作为监督信号。

Result: 在通用抓取任务的真实机器人实验中，AVP 的成功率较 pi_0.5 提升了 27.61%，在数据效率、空间组合泛化和对象级迁移方面表现优于现有最新方法。

Conclusion: AVP 通过引入视觉原语接口，成功解耦了感知与动作，提升了机器人操作的成功率、训练效率及泛化能力，证明了该范式在通用机器人操纵中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [144] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶中因遮挡导致的不确定性，本文提出一种统一的风险图建模与学习框架，结合扩散模型生成的对抗性场景，显著提升了部分观测环境下的风险评估精度与规控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶遮挡预测方法存在两个问题：一是因过度保守而高估风险导致通行效率低下，二是难以在高遮挡不确定性下准确预测轨迹。

Method: 1. 提出一种集成了交通流风险与碰撞风险的时空统一风险图建模与学习框架；2. 引入基于扩散模型的场景生成框架，生成真实且具有判别力的遮挡交互场景以缓解数据稀缺问题；3. 将风险图整合至风险感知规控流程中。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有SOTA遮挡预测基线，最小碰撞时间（MTTC）提升了0.78倍，平均碰撞时间（ATTC）提升了1.67倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一风险图模型与学习框架有效提升了自动驾驶在部分观测环境下的风险感知与规控能力，为解决遮挡带来的不确定性提供了实用且全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [145] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine2Real 提出了一种无几何约束的零样本全身人机交互框架，通过将动作统一为 4D 点轨迹并利用行为基础模型约束稀疏关键点追踪，有效规避了传统动作重定向的复杂性和模型失调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全身人机交互（HOI）研究受限于高精度 3D 数据稀缺。基于视频生成先验的方法在处理几何模型时存在显著表示失调，且现有重定向方法计算复杂、误差易累积。

Method: 1. 统一表征：将机器人与对象运动抽象为统一的 4D 点轨迹，消除几何失调。
2. 稀疏关键点追踪：通过追踪基座、手部和对象的稀疏关键点，完全规避复杂的动作重定向（Retargeting）。
3. 行为先验引导：利用行为基础模型（BFM）的潜在空间作为搜索域，约束稀疏信号下的动作生成。
4. 渐进式训练：采用简单的奖励机制进行训练，实现物理环境零样本部署。

Result: 该框架实现了端到端的零样本物理部署，无需复杂的几何建模或动作重定向技术，同时保持了人形机器人交互动作的自然度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Imagine2Real 成功通过动作基础模型实现无几何约束的零样本人机交互，解决了传统方法中模型失调与重定向复杂性的痛点，在简化部署流程的同时保证了动作的自然性。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process.
