Across vision tasks and large language model pretraining, AMUSE consistently improves the performance-iteration Pareto frontier over (Schedule-Free) AdamW and Muon.

</details>


### [252] [Don't Forget the Critic: Value-Based Data Rehearsal for Multi-Cyclic Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22454)
*Benjamin Poole,Andrew Quinn,Li Yang,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持续强化学习中值函数正则化被忽视的问题，本文提出了 Qreg+NWLU，通过持续更新样本集和即时正则化手段，有效解决了多周期环境下值函数近似导致的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续强化学习（CRL）研究多局限于策略梯度框架（仅正则化 Actor），忽略了值函数正则化的潜力；此外，现有研究缺乏对“多周期环境”（任务序列重复）的评估，而这类环境更容易导致遗忘和可塑性问题。

Method: 提出了 Qreg+NWLU 模型，包含两个改进：(1)“持续数据回放”，在训练过程中动态收集并更新存储的 Q 值；(2)“无等待正则化”，从首个任务开始立即应用正则化，而非滞后执行。

Result: Qreg+NWLU 在值函数近似设置下，相比于常规的 Qreg 及其他传统 CRL 方法，在学习效率、遗忘抑制及知识迁移方面表现出显著改进。

Conclusion: 在多周期持续强化学习环境中，通过持续数据回放和“无等待”正则化，Qreg+NWLU 能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘，提升样本效率和跨任务的知识迁移能力，证明了值函数正则化在 CRL 中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Data rehearsal has emerged as a leading approach for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL). However, existing work remains confined to policy gradient frameworks, regularizing only actors due to the performance degradation incurred by critic regularization. This actor-centric approach overlooks the potential of data rehearsal for value function approximation. Moreover, existing evaluations in CRL rarely consider multi-cyclic environments where task sequences repeat, a critical real-world scenario that exacerbates forgetting and plasticity. We investigate data rehearsal for Deep Q-Networks using Q-value regularization in multi-cyclic settings and propose Qreg+NWLU which introduces two simple modifications: (1) continuous data rehearsal that dynamically collects and updates stored Q-values throughout training, and (2) "No-Wait" regularization that applies immediately rather than after the first task. Together, these modifications yield improvements in learning efficiency, forgetting mitigation, and knowledge transfer over Qreg and conventional CRL methods within value function approximation settings.

</details>


### [253] [The Signal in the Noise: OOD Detection Through Goodness-of-Fit Testing in Factorised Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22496)
*Philipp Bomatter,Jack Geary,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生成模型似然函数在 OOD 检测上的失效，提出 SITN 方法，通过分析连续归一化流的噪声空间分布特征，在无需额外训练数据的情况下实现了精确的 OOD 检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度生成模型在分布外（OOD）检测中表现出的不确定性问题，特别是似然函数在区分分布内（ID）与 OOD 数据时不可靠，以及传统方法中存在的“复杂度偏差（complexity bias）”问题。

Method: 提出“Signal in the Noise (SITN)”方法。利用连续归一化流（CNF）的微分同胚与质量守恒特性，分析 OOD 样本在潜在空间中映射到的噪声分布，识别出传统似然函数无法捕获的典型噪声特征（即并非所有“高似然”样本都是分布内数据）。

Result: 在标准基准测试和合成扰动实验中表现出色；无需 OOD 数据进行训练，计算开销极小，且能对假阳性率（FPR）进行严格控制。

Conclusion: SITN 证明了通过监测连续归一化流（CNF）在潜在空间中的噪声分布特征，可以有效克服深度生成模型在 OOD 检测中对似然函数的过度依赖，且不会受到传统方法中“复杂度偏差”的影响。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a natural foundation for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet prior work has shown that their assigned likelihoods are notoriously unreliable indicators for in- vs out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we address this problem by leveraging the diffeomorphic and mass-preserving properties of continuous normalising flows. Our analysis shows that OOD samples are mapped to noise samples that are highly atypical under the noise prior in ways not captured by the likelihood. Based on this observation, we propose a new method -- Signal in the Noise (SITN) -- for OOD detection on the single-sample level. SITN requires no access to OOD data, incurs minimal computational overhead, and provides strict control of false positive rates. Comprehensive evaluations through standard benchmarks and synthetic perturbations highlight the method's effectiveness and the absence of the complexity bias inherent to likelihood-based methods.

</details>


### [254] [Disentanglement Beyond Generative Models with Riemannian ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22531)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有解耦理论过度依赖全局生成模型的局限，本文提出了“黎曼ICA (RICA)”，通过局部黎曼几何和解耦张量，实现了在无需坐标依赖和全局假设下的局部解耦分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦理论严重依赖全局生成模型（如非线性ICA），但在现代预训练表示学习中，这种强假设难以成立。因此需要一种无需全局生成假设、能解释特征如何作为独立变化因素的通用理论。

Method: 提出了“黎曼独立成分分析 (RICA)”，通过局部几何结构替代全局生成模型；引入“解耦张量 (Disentanglement Tensor)”，利用数据对数似然的 Hessian 矩阵和诱导的 Ricci 曲率定义“点解耦 (Pointwise Disentanglement)”。

Result: 在受控源恢复实验中，RICA 成功在多个流形上恢复了源信号，且其性能不受观测坐标系选择的影响，优于传统的 ICA 基线方法。

Conclusion: RICA 通过引入黎曼几何，为在不依赖全局生成模型的情况下研究局部解耦提供了理论基础，提出了一种独立于观测坐标系的解耦度量方式。

Abstract: There is a gap between the theoretical foundations of disentanglement and the practice of modern representation learning. Existing theoretical frameworks, particularly Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and its nonlinear variants, assume a generative model with statistically independent latent variables underlying the data so that disentanglement amounts to identifying the latents that could have generated the data. This generative framework is interpretable and theoretically justified, but its strong assumptions make it difficult to apply to modern representation learning. Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [255] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的高效方法，用于验证语言模型中“关系线性”的假设，研究表明该属性在不同模型层级和查询表述下表现出明显的差异性和不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型中线性属性的研究缺乏有效且精确的实验评估方法。本文旨在验证Marconato等人提出的“关系线性”假设——即给定关系，对象的反嵌入（unembedding）可以通过主体的嵌入（embedding）经过线性映射预测得到。

Method: 提出了一种基于KL散度（Kullback-Leibler divergence）的探测方法（probing method），用于评估关系线性（relational linearity），该方法相比以往基于雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）近似的手段更高效。

Result: 通过四个数据集的实验发现：关系线性在不同模型间表现各异，在模型层级中呈现出与语言信息处理一致的模式，且对关系查询的表述方式（paraphrasing）变化表现出不同的敏感度。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了关系线性假设确实存在，但其稳健性受到模型层面、层级位置以及查询措辞方式的多重影响。研究提出的评估方法在效率和科学性上优于以往的方法。

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [256] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型强化学习（GRPO）在去中心化训练中因模型异质性导致的离线数据收敛难题，本文提出 F-TIS 算法，在保证通信效率的同时实现了异构模型的高效协作与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GRPO 方法在分布式训练中通常假设模型同质以保持在线策略，这在计算资源异构的去中心化系统中不切实际；而处理异质模型会导致产生破坏训练收敛的离线样本。

Method: 提出了“过滤截断重要性采样”（Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling, F-TIS），这是一种专为异构模型设计的强化学习训练范式，旨在有效处理分布式系统中的离线样本，并保持通信效率。

Result: 实验表明，F-TIS 在多种异构设置下均能表现出与纯在线采样训练一致的收敛性，并在部分外推（OOD）任务上性能提升高达 12%。

Conclusion: F-TIS 通过允许异构模型协作，解决了 GRPO 在去中心化训练中因离线采样导致的收敛问题，实现了与在线采样一致或更好的模型性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [257] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ImplicitTerrainV2 是一种新型紧凑型隐式神经地形表示，通过小波引导的空间自适应机制和导数感知监督，实现了在极低比特率下的高保真地形重构及高效导数分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的栅格 DEM 依赖内插和有限差分分析，而现有的隐式神经表示 (INR) 缺乏频率控制、忽视了地形的梯度结构，且模型规模大、训练成本高，难以实用化。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ImplicitTerrainV2 的方法，主要技术包含：1. 小波复杂度场 (WCF) 以实现空间自适应频率控制；2. 基于复杂度的自适应采样；3. 导数感知监督（Gradient-aware supervision）以保持地形平滑流形结构；4. 后训练混合精度量化与熵编码压缩。

Result: 在 50 个瑞士地形瓦片数据上，ImplicitTerrainV2 实现了 66.25 dB 的 PSNR，比前序工作提升 5.70 dB，参数量减少 3.2 倍，单瓦片训练时间仅 55 秒。压缩后达到 1.23 bpp，且支持任意位置查询和闭式导数计算。

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2 提供了一种高效、紧凑、支持连续查询且具备高地形导数保真度的神经地形表示，在性能和压缩率上均优于现有的地形表示方法，具备极高的 GIS 应用潜力。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [258] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种利用离线GCRL中层次结构实现空间“绝对抽象”的方法，通过相对化选项和多级表征来消除冗余并提升学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的目标条件强化学习常因状态-目标对的对称性和结构共享导致冗余，且现有的层次策略多用于时域抽象（降低视界），该文旨在利用层次结构实现空间的“绝对抽象”以消除冗余。

Method: 提出了一种基于层次强化学习的框架，引入了“相对化选项”（relativised options）和针对不同层次的差异化表征，将绝对坐标系转化为相对坐标系，从而促进跨状态空间的经验重用。

Result: 基于该框架设计的两种算法在实验中证明，通过引入这种归纳偏置，显著提升了离线GCRL的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入相对化选项和层次化的层次表征，利用环境的对称性和结构共享，能够有效提升离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）的样本效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [259] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 GP-UCB 提出了一种理论完备的停止准则，通过更紧的瞬时后悔界（instantaneous regret bounds），确保在达到指定的 ε-最优精度时自动停止优化，解决了传统贝叶斯优化中固定预算带来的效率和质量保障难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）通常依靠固定预算（fixed evaluation budget）停止，导致效率低下或无法保证解的质量，缺乏理论上完备的停止机制是该领域长期存在的挑战。

Method: 1. 推导并提出了 GP-UCB 在任意迭代步数下的更紧的上界（instantaneous regret bounds）；2. 基于该理论界设计了新的停止准则，确保最终解在置信概率 1-δ 下为 ε-最优解。

Result: 数值实验证明，所提出的停止准则不仅在理论上更严谨，而且在实际应用中能有效平衡计算成本与求解质量，实现了优化的自动终止。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对 GP-UCB 的理论完备的停止准则，能够在满足预定精度要求（ε-optimal）且具有高概率保证（1-δ）的前提下，实现贝叶斯优化的自动停止，从而避免不必要的计算损耗。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [260] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS)来实现高效的全协方差匹配，将DDPM的路径KL误差从O(1/T)降低到O(1/T^2)，显著提升了快速采样的质量与理论性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯DDPM中标准的各项同性（各向同性）反向协方差会导致不可避免的O(1/T)路径KL误差，这在需要干预整个反向轨迹的方法（如分类器引导）中尤为严重。本文旨在通过匹配全后验协方差来打破这一误差壁垒。

Method: 1. 证明匹配全后验协方差可将路径KL误差从O(1/T)降低至O(1/T^2)；2. 提出Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS)：一种训练无关（training-free）、矩阵无关（matrix-free）的方法，利用Jacobian-vector积执行全协方差采样；3. 证明LGS的近似误差随Lanczos步数呈指数级衰减。

Result: LGS仅需3个Lanczos步数即可改善采样质量，在多个标准图像基准测试中优于强基线模型（如OCM-DDPM）；同时避免了存储密集协方差矩阵或训练辅助协方差模型的需求。

Conclusion: 全协方差匹配不仅在理论上具有重要价值，而且对于加速DDPM采样而言是切实可行的。通过Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS)，可以在不增加额外辅助模型的情况下，打破传统对角协方差在路径KL误差上的限制。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [261] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EMO-STA 框架，利用跨任务共享演化机制优化 LLM 程序发现，通过平衡共享与适应过程，在提升搜索效率与泛化能力的同时，有效解决了单任务优化导致的过拟合和计算冗余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 引导演化搜索方法通常独立优化每个任务，未能利用任务间的结构重叠，导致计算效率低下且在少量数据下易产生针对特定任务的过拟合。

Method: 提出了 EMO-STA（Shared-Then-Adapt，先共享后适应）两阶段框架：1. 在任务族上演化出一个共享的程序库；2. 将最优的共享程序适应转化至各个特定目标任务。探讨了多种适应策略（如温启动、最佳平均共享、任务内最佳共享适应）。

Result: 在八个任务族（涵盖连续优化、几何构造、建模等）的测试中，EMO-STA 在多数情况下优于同等计算量的单任务演化；Best-Local 策略在同分布适应中表现最佳，Best-Shared 策略在未见任务表现出强泛化能力；此外，该方法显著缓解了低样本环境下的过拟合现象。

Conclusion: EMO-STA 是一个有效的多任务程序发现框架，通过跨任务共享演化能够增强程序发现的通用性、效率及稳健性，有效缓解过拟合问题，优于传统的单任务演化。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [262] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CogAdapt 框架通过 LeadBridge 适配器与 ProFine 渐进式微调策略，成功将临床 ECG 基础模型迁移至可穿戴认知负荷评估任务，有效解决了导联差异与泛化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知负荷评估面临标签数据稀缺及跨被试泛化能力弱的问题；尽管临床 ECG 基础模型表现优异，但因导联配置差异及任务不匹配，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出了 CogAdapt 框架，包含两个核心组件：1. LeadBridge：通过可学习适配器将 3 导联可穿戴信号转换为临床 12 导联表征；2. ProFine：一种渐进式微调策略，旨在逐步解冻编码器层以防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 CLARE 和 CL-Drive 公共数据集上进行留一法（LOSO）交叉验证，CogAdapt 的宏 F1 分数分别达到 0.626 和 0.768，全面优于从零训练的基准方法。

Conclusion: CogAdapt 证明了通过适当的适配策略，将临床 ECG 基础模型应用于可穿戴设备认知负荷评估是高效且可行的，显著提升了跨被试的泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [263] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了 Wasserstein 策略优化（WPO）在熵正则化 MDP 环境下具有线性收敛性，填补了其在连续空间内收敛性分析的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Wasserstein 策略优化（WPO）算法在连续动作空间的强化学习中表现出色，但其在连续状态和动作空间中的理论收敛特性尚不明确。

Method: 利用均值场分析（Mean-field analysis）和对数-Sobolev不等式（log-Sobolev inequalities）研究梯度流的收敛性；假设梯度流方程存在足够平滑的解，证明了能量的单调耗散及局部对数-Sobolev不等式的成立。

Result: 证明了在上述假设条件下，WPO 的值函数能够线性收敛至全局最优解。

Conclusion: 在熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下，WPO 算法在连续状态和动作空间中能够实现线性收敛。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [264] [Why SGD is not Brownian Motion: A New Perspective on Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22644)
*Igor Ignashin,Anna Radovskaya,Andrew Semenov,Egor Lopatin,Stanislav Potapov,Aleksandr Kovalenko,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Shestakov,Andrey Leonidov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散动力学而非连续朗之万近似的SGD分析框架，揭示了SGD在有限学习率下会导致平坦方向上的有效扩散，而非简单的参数漂移或固定分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将SGD建模为布朗运动的朗之万近似依赖于连续时间极限，且噪声缩放与有限学习率下的离散更新不匹配，因此需要一种更准确描述离散SGD动力学的框架。

Method: 从随机梯度下降（SGD）的离散更新规则出发，推导参数分布的主方程（Master Equation），并导出一个在O(eta^2)阶上不同于标准朗之万方程的离散福克-普朗克方程。通过均值海森矩阵的特征基分解，分析SGD在临界点附近的动力学特性。

Result: 发现了SGD动力学在均值海森矩阵特征基上的分解特性；揭示了平坦方向上，参数方差随时间增长，且扩散系数与学习率成正比，从而区分了受限模式与扩散模式。实验验证了该理论在计算机视觉和NLP模型中的有效性。

Conclusion: SGD离散动力学的行为不能完全由连续时间朗之万方程描述，特别是在学习率有限时，存在由小批量采样导致的特定扩散效应。在平坦方向上，参数分布不服从固定分布，而是表现出随时间增长的扩散现象。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) is commonly modeled as a Langevin process, assuming that minibatch noise acts as Brownian motion. However, this approximation relies on a continuous-time limit and a sqrt(eta) noise scaling that does not match the discrete SGD update at finite learning rate. In this work, we propose an alternative formulation of SGD as deterministic dynamics in a fluctuating loss landscape induced by minibatch sampling. Starting directly from the discrete update, we derive a master equation for the parameter distribution and obtain a discrete Fokker--Planck equation that differs from the standard Langevin form at order eta^2. Using this framework, we analyze SGD dynamics near critical points of the loss. We show that the behavior decomposes along the eigenbasis of the mean Hessian into qualitatively distinct regimes. In particular, nearly-flat directions do not admit a stationary distribution: the variance grows over time, corresponding to effective diffusion along valleys with a coefficient proportional to the learning rate. We provide empirical evidence supporting these predictions on neural network models in computer vision and natural language processing, observing a clear qualitative separation between confined and diffusive modes.

</details>


### [265] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过朗道稳定性分析揭示了$\beta$-VAE中后验坍缩的本质是自动谱剪枝，展示了潜变量模式随$\beta$增加而呈级联式坍缩的机制，并在线性情况下将其与PCA谱建立了严格对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$\beta$-VAE中常见的“后验坍缩”（posterior collapse）现象，旨在从理论上解释为何某些潜变量模式会失效，并揭示$\beta$参数如何通过类似于物理学中相变或谱剪枝的机制控制潜变量的表征能力。

Method: 1. 采用朗道稳定性分析（Landau stability analysis）推导出损失函数的稳定性条件；2. 定义了一个潜变量重缩放不变参数（latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter）来对活动潜变量模式进行排序；3. 在线性高斯模型下，将崩溃谱（collapse spectrum）与PCA谱进行数学等价性证明；4. 使用WorldClim数据集进行实证验证。

Result: 1. 证明了在$\beta$-VAE中，后验坍缩遵循明确的阈值逻辑；2. 揭示了在线性高斯情况下，崩溃谱、效用谱与标准化PCA谱的一致性；3. 验证了坍缩过程遵循平均场律（mean-field law）。

Conclusion: $\beta$-VAE中的后验坍缩本质上是一种自动谱剪枝（automatic spectral pruning），潜变量模式的坍缩受$\beta$值设定的阈值控制，反映了潜变量从无用信息到有用信息的层级解耦过程。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [266] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 RLVR 中 GRPO 算法因强制硬裁剪导致有效信息丢失的瓶颈，提出 NSR 方法，通过在边界处进行随机保留（而非一刀切丢弃），有效提升了 LLM 推理任务的训练稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（特别是基于裁剪的 GRPO 目标函数）中硬裁剪策略会导致位于裁剪边界附近的有效信息被丢弃，造成训练不稳定及收敛性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出“近边界随机抢救”（Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue, NSR）方法，通过引入随机扰动机制，在训练过程中有条件地保留超出裁剪阈值（near-boundary）的信息，而非完全丢弃。

Result: 在 7B 到 30B 参数规模及不同架构（Dense/MoE）的实验中，NSR 均表现出优于 DAPO 和 GSPO 等基准的性能，且具有良好的通用性与即插即用特性。

Conclusion: NSR 通过在边界处执行随机保留机制，解决了 GRPO 类目标函数中硬裁剪带来的信息丢失问题，作为一种即插即用的优化方案，显著提升了 RLVR 的训练稳定性和模型推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [267] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种廉价、模型无关的审计流水线，用于评估模型行为背后的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征。通过对 GPT-2 进行实证研究，证明了许多看似相关的特征并非模型行为的真正因果驱动力，强调了在神经可解释性研究中进行严格因果校验的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征与模型在特定任务（间接宾语识别 IOI）执行中的失败与成功是否存在显著关联，并评估这些特征是否为真正的机制驱动因素。

Method: 通过对 GPT-2 Small 在 IOI 任务上的 300 个提示进行测试，使用 Bloom (2024) 的层 8 残差流 SAE 特征进行统计审计（Holm 校正、Cohen's d），并引入因果消融（Causal Ablation）、逻辑回归表示基准以及多随机种子鲁棒性检查进行三项控制实验。

Result: 审计发现了与执行失败强烈相关的特征（如“cryptographic keys”），但在进一步因果消融实验中发现其并非失败的根本驱动因素（非充分条件）。实验表明 SAE 提供了可解释性而非额外的预测能力，且单特征的相关性经常缺乏跨随机种子的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该论文强调了审计流水线（Audit Pipeline）的价值，而非单一特征的发现。尽管稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征在解释模型行为上具有潜力，但必须通过严格的因果性分析、基准测试和鲁棒性校验来验证其有效性。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [268] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树，通过在分裂节点引入自适应不确定性区域，在处理边界模糊样本时改用加权预测，从而显著提高了决策树的准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬阈值进行空间划分，无法区分距离决策边界远近的样本，导致边界附近的预测置信度缺失，在关键应用场景中缺乏预警能力。

Method: 提出三元决策树模型，即在每个分裂节点设置关于最优阈值的“不确定性区域”（宽度为delta）。当数据点落入该区域时，采用左右子树预测值的加权融合，并将其标记为边界不确定。研究提出了五种delta估计方法：质量高原、类别重叠、增益比、节点自举法和边缘（Margin）法。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上的验证表明，所有五种方法均显著优于标准CART的决定准确率（p < 0.001）。其中“边缘（Margin）”方法表现最佳，无需额外超参数且效率最高。此外，在医疗和金融领域的应用证明了该方法通过标记不确定样本有效提升了整体准确性。

Conclusion: 三元决策树（Ternary Decision Trees）通过在切分节点引入不确定性区域，有效地处理了决策边界附近的预测模糊性。这种方法无需额外超参数，能够显著提升CART决策树的准确性，并为实际应用场景（如医疗诊断）提供可靠的边界不确定性预警。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [269] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SeqLoRA，一种利用双层优化和持续学习解决多概念扩散模型微调中干扰问题的框架，实现了高效、高扩展性且不牺牲保真度的多概念合成，支持最多101个概念的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型微调方法在合成多个自定义概念时，常面临“表征干扰”（representation interference）问题。现有模块化方法在昂贵的后验融合与限制表达能力的冻结子空间之间难以平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于双层优化（bilevel optimization）的限制性持续学习框架 SeqLoRA。该方法同步优化 LoRA 因子的权重，并引入了基于矩阵次高斯过程（matrix sub-Gaussian process）的理论分析，用于约束参数更新并减少灾难性遗忘。

Result: 实验证明 SeqLoRA 在多概念图像生成任务中显著优于基线方法，在保持高保真度的同时，避免了高昂的融合成本，并有效降低了属性冲突（减少了干扰能量）。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA 通过联合优化 LoRA 权重和正则化机制，有效解决了多概念合成中的表征干扰问题，在插件式多概念生成任务中表现出优异的身份保持能力和扩展性（最高支持101个概念）。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [270] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有好奇心驱动RL在复杂3D环境中易陷入局部循环的问题，本文提出了ReCuriosity，通过结合在线3D重建与序列模型，赋予智能体空间持久性和情节记忆，实现了高效的自主探索与卓越的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动的强化学习在复杂、逼真的3D环境中表现不佳，智能体常陷入局部循环（Local Loops），主要原因是缺乏对环境的空间持久性认知和长期情节记忆。

Method: 引入在线3D重建作为世界的持久化模型，解决空间持久性问题；利用序列模型处理RGB观察序列，赋予智能体记忆能力（Episodic Context）；采用纯好奇心驱动的强化学习进行训练。

Result: 智能体在HM3D数据集的探索中超越了基于主动绘图（Active Mapping）的基准算法，并在未见过的Gibson环境及AI生成的世界中展现出零样本泛化能力，在下游任务（如苹果采摘和图像目标导航）中显著优于从零开始训练的基准方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于持久化3D重建和序列模型的策略框架（ReCuriosity），能够有效解决复杂3D环境中好奇心驱动探索导致的“局部循环”问题，实现了高效的自主探索，并具有极强的零样本泛化能力和下游任务适应性。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Bridging the Cold-Start Gap: LLM-Powered Synthetic Data Generation for Natural Language Search at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21812)
*Wendy Ran Wei,Hao Li,Weiwei Guo,Xiaowei Liu,Xueyin Chen,Dillon Davis,Malay Haldar,Soumyadip Banerjee,Kedar Bellare,Huiji Gao,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM构建合成对比查询和标签的流水线，有效解决了搜索系统的冷启动问题，并在查询分布模拟和模型评估性能上显著优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索系统在初始部署时面临严重的“冷启动”问题：缺乏真实的用户查询以学习语言模式，且缺乏相关性标签以训练排序模型。

Method: 提出了一个利用大语言模型（LLM）生成合成搜索流水线的框架：1. 查询生成：结合预订会话中的对比列表对与用户研究中的种子查询，以平衡现实性和多样性；2. 标签生成：引入“对比生成”构建主题相关性标签，并使用“虚拟评审员（Virtual Judge）”机制提升覆盖率。

Result: 相比基准方法，该方法在查询长度分布上KL散度从12.03降至0.66（提升7.5倍），在属性分布上以0.04的KL散度优于种子查询（0.09）；生成的评估集更具区分度（成对准确率从97%降至79%），现已应用于Airbnb的生产环境。

Conclusion: 通过构造的合成查询和标签，该框架有效解决了搜索系统在冷启动阶段缺乏用户数据和标注数据的难题，并能随着真实数据的积累实现从冷启动到热启动的平滑过渡。

Abstract: Deploying natural language search systems presents a critical cold-start challenge: no real user queries to learn linguistic patterns, and no relevance labels to train ranking models. We present a framework for generating synthetic queries and labels using large language models (LLMs), powering model training and evaluation for Airbnb's natural language search.
  For query generation, we combine contrastive listing pairs from booking sessions with seed queries from user research to balance realism and diversity, enabling a cold-to-warm start transition as real user data becomes available. For label generation, we introduce contrastive generation that produces topicality labels by construction, and Virtual Judge (VJ) labeling for broader coverage.
  We compare our approach against a no-seed contrastive baseline and an InPars-style baseline. For query length, the InPars baseline produces verbose queries with KL divergence of 12.03 vs. real users; our seed-guided approach achieves 0.66, a 7.5x improvement. For attribute type distributions, our approach achieves the lowest KL divergence (0.04), outperforming even seed queries (0.09). Experiments show our approach produces harder evaluation examples than the no-seed baseline (79% vs. 97% pairwise accuracy), providing discriminative signal for model improvement. We deploy production pipelines generating synthetic examples daily for embedding-based retrieval and ranking evaluation.

</details>


### [272] [Reinforced Preference Optimization for Reasoning-Augmented Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21967)
*Jingtong Gao,Zeyu Song,Chi Lu,Xiaopeng Li,Derong Xu,Maolin Wang,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RPORec 框架，通过两阶段的强化偏好优化，有效地将 LLM 的推理逻辑与推荐系统的商品检索任务对齐，显著提升了推荐准确性和解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的推荐方法在将 LLM 的推理过程与推荐目标对齐方面存在困难，包括结构破坏和自由形式生成难以转化为精确商品预测的问题。

Method: 提出 RPORec 框架，包含两个阶段：1. 推理增强推荐建模（利用 CoT 生成辅助知识引导 Rechead）；2. 高级推理精炼与对齐（通过 Rechead 生成可验证奖励，利用强化学习微调 LLM）。

Result: 在公共基准测试和大规模线上部署中，RPORec 均表现优于当前最先进的基于 LLM 的推荐方法。

Conclusion: RPORec 通过将 LLM 的推理能力与专注推荐的模块（Rechead）统一，实现了更精准的个性化推荐，证明了推理增强在推荐系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Recommender systems are critical for delivering personalized content across digital platforms, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to enhance them with richer world knowledge and explicit reasoning capabilities. With the help of reasoning knowledge, recommendations can better infer users' underlying intents, adapt to evolving preferences, and leverage semantic relationships for improved accuracy and interpretability. However, existing reasoning-based recommendation methods often fail to fully align the LLM's reasoning process with recommendation-specific objectives due to structural disruption during integration and difficulties in translating free-form generation into accurate item predictions. In this paper, we introduce RPORec, a reinforced preference optimization framework that unifies an LLM backbone's reasoning ability with a dedicated recommendation head (Rechead) for precise item retrieval. RPORec comprises two stages: (1) Reasoning-Augmented Recommendation Modeling, where high-quality Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning is generated and used as auxiliary knowledge to guide the Rechead in learning recommendation-specific representations; and (2) Advanced Reasoning Refinement and Alignment, in which the trained Rechead produces verifiable rewards to fine-tune the LLM backbone via reinforcement learning, enhancing reasoning quality, structural consistency, and task relevance. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and large-scale online deployments show that RPORec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based recommendation methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-augmented recommendation modeling in real-world systems.

</details>


### [273] [LLM Retrieval for Stable and Predictable Ad Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21969)
*Vinodh Kumar Sunkara,Satheeshkumar Karuppusamy,Hangjun Xu,Sai Deepika Regani,Kshitij Gupta,Gaby Nahum,Sneha Iyer,Jean-Baptiste Fiot,Yinglong Guo,Xiaowen Guo,Atul Jangra,Yucheng Liu,Jinghao Yan,Vijay Pappu,Benjamin Schulte,Deepak Chandra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对广告推荐系统在生成式AI环境下的不稳定性问题，引入了一种基于LLM的语义候选生成框架，通过增强语义感知能力，在提升系统预测稳定性的同时也优化了传统推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI导致广告库存和流动性激增，传统侧重于点击/转化准确率的推荐系统在预测稳定性和可预测性方面面临挑战，导致广告投放出现不可预知、冷启动及探索不足等问题。

Method: 提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）的在线验证语义候选生成框架。具体包括：1. 从广告创意中提取分层语义属性以获取LLM表示；2. 利用这些表示进行基于图的扩展，确保候选集能够捕捉广告语义变体，从而实现对小扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 在线下和在线A/B测试中均显示出显著提升，不仅改善了系统的可预测性，还提升了NDCG等传统核心性能指标。

Conclusion: 通过引入大模型增强语义感知，本文提出的框架在提升广告推荐系统预测稳定性与可预测性的同时，也兼顾了传统的点击/转化性能指标，为大规模推荐系统提供了一种通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional ads recommendation systems have primarily focused on optimizing for prediction accuracy of click or conversion events using canonical metrics such as recall or normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG). With the hyper-growth of ads inventory and liquidity with generative AI technologies, the prediction stability and predictability is becoming increasingly critical. Intuitively, prediction stability and predictability can be defined to quantify system robustness with respect to minor/noisy input (ads, creatives) perturbations, the lack of which could lead to advertiser perceivable problems such as repeatability, cold start and under-exploration. In this paper, we introduce a new evaluation framework for quantifying stability and predictability of an ads recommender system, and present an online validated semantic candidate generation framework powered by fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) that showed significant improvement along these metrics by fundamentally improving the semantic-awareness of the system. The approach extracts hierarchical semantic attributes from ad creatives to obtain LLM representations, which serve as the foundation for graph-based expansion, ensuring the retrieved candidates encapsulate semantic variants of an ad, guaranteeing that small creative variants from the advertiser yield consistent and explainable delivery results to the user. We tested this LLM ads retrieval framework in a large-scale industrial ads recommendation system, demonstrating significant improvements across offline and online A/B experiments, showcasing gains in both predictability and traditional performance metrics. Although evaluated in the ads stack, this is a general framework that can be applied broadly to any large-scale recommendation and retrieval systems facing similar scaling and predictability challenges.

</details>


### [274] [Generative Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21987)
*Sixiao Zhang,Mingrui Liu,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将推荐和对话生成统一于单一自回归框架的生成式会话推荐系统，通过结构化生成范式优化了意图建模与推荐决策，大幅提升了推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有会话推荐系统通常将推荐与对话生成解耦，或者依赖于基于检索的管道，这导致了推荐与生成过程缺乏整合，难以有效建模用户意图，从而限制了系统的性能。

Method: 提出了一种完全生成式的会话推荐系统：1. 将商品表示为离散语义 ID 并纳入单一自回归框架；2. 设计了一种结构化生成范式，通过“先预测意图与推荐目标，后生成回复”的两阶段过程来强制执行依赖结构；3. 结合约束解码（constrained decoding）实现准确的商品生成。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现出色，在 Recall@1 指标上相比强基线模型提升了 29%，同时保持了高质量的对话生成能力。

Conclusion: 通过提出的统一自回归架构和结构化生成范式，该方法显着提升了推荐性能与对话连贯性，证明了将推荐与对话生成深度结合的有效性。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems aim to provide personalized recommendations via natural language interactions. However, existing approaches either decouple recommendation from dialog generation or rely on retrieval-based pipelines, limiting the integration between recommendation and response generation and leading to suboptimal modeling of user intent. In this paper, we propose a fully generative conversational recommender system that unifies recommendation and dialog generation within a single autoregressive framework. Our approach represents items as discrete semantic IDs and integrates them directly into the generation process, enabling joint prediction of items and responses via next-token modeling. We further introduce a structured generation paradigm that factorizes conversational recommendation into a sequence of interdependent decisions, where the model first predicts the response intent and the recommendation target, and then generates the response conditioned on them. This design enables end-to-end optimization, enforces a more coherent dependency structure, and supports faithful item generation via constrained decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves recommendation performance, achieving gains of up to 29% on Recall@1 over strong baselines, while maintaining competitive dialog quality.

</details>


### [275] [Integrating Chain-of-Thought into Generative Retrieval: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22358)
*Wenhao Zhang,Ruihao Yu,Yi Bai,Zhumin Chen,Pengjie Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ThinkGR 是一个为生成式检索引入思维链（CoT）的统一框架。通过混合解码策略和两阶段强化学习训练，显著提升了模型在复杂多跳检索任务中的推理与检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式检索方法通常直接将查询映射至文档 ID，缺乏中间推理过程，难以应对需要多步推理的复杂查询。

Method: 提出 ThinkGR 框架，包含：1. 混合解码策略（动态切换自由思维生成与受限文档 ID 解码）；2. 两阶段训练方案（首先通过监督微调对齐思维-检索模式，随后利用基于检索的强化学习优化思维质量）。

Result: 在四个多跳检索基准测试中达到了最先进的性能，平均提升了 6.86%。

Conclusion: ThinkGR 通过引入思维链（CoT）赋予生成式检索（GR）显式的推理能力，有效地解决了复杂多跳检索任务中的挑战，为检索领域的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: While generative retrieval (GR) demonstrates competitive performance on standard retrieval benchmarks, existing approaches directly map queries to document identifiers (docids) without intermediate deliberation, limiting their effectiveness for complex queries that require multi-step reasoning. As a preliminary study on integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) into generative retrieval, we introduce ThinkGR, a unified framework that interleaves CoT with docid generation, enabling iterative thinking and retrieval within a single generative process. To bridge the gap between free-form thought generation and structured retrieval targets, we design (1) a hybrid decoding strategy that dynamically switches between unconstrained thought generation and constrained docid decoding, and (2) a two-phase training approach that first aligns thought-retrieval patterns through supervised fine-tuning, then optimizes thought quality via retrieval-grounded reinforcement learning. Experiments on four multi-hop retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkGR achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average improvement of +6.86\%. Our work opens new avenues for enhancing generative retrieval with explicit deliberation capabilities, with promising implications for retrieval tasks requiring complex reasoning.

</details>


### [276] [Diversed Model Discovery via Structured Table Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22766)
*Zhengyuan Dong,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有模型搜索依赖文本导致缺乏对比性的问题，本文提出了 StructuredSemanticSearch 框架，通过引入表格发现技术和基于碎片（nugget）的评估协议，实现了更精准、更多样化的模型检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型搜索系统过于依赖语义文本相似度，导致结果同质化且缺乏可量化的对比。研究者认为模型检索本质上是对比性的，核心信息往往蕴含在模型卡的结构化表格中而非冗长的文本描述里。

Method: 提出 StructuredSemanticSearch 框架，利用表格发现算子（联合、连接、关键词匹配）从模型卡中提取结构化证据，并设计了“面向方向的集成（orientation-aware integration）”来处理多源表格。同时，构建了基于“信息碎片（nugget）”的评估协议，用于衡量证据覆盖度和多样性。

Result: 在包含597个查询的实验中，StructuredSemanticSearch 在证据覆盖度（nugget coverage）和多样性指标上均显著优于传统的语义搜索基准。

Conclusion: StructuredSemanticSearch 框架通过整合结构化表格数据和语义搜索，能比单纯的语义基准更有效地覆盖用户查询的具体信息，显著提升了模型检索的查准率和多样性。

Abstract: Model cards describe model behavior through a mixture of textual descriptions and structured artifacts, including performance, configuration, and dataset tables. Existing model search systems rely predominantly on semantic similarity over text, which can produce homogeneous result sets and limit exploration of alternatives. We argue that model search is inherently comparative: users want models that are task-aligned yet differentiated in measurable ways. We hypothesize that this balance requires retrieval over condensed, high-quality evidence rather than verbose descriptions, and much of that evidence is concentrated in structured tables. We present StructuredSemanticSearch, a table-driven model search framework built on the ModelTables benchmark. Given a query, StructuredSemanticSearch combines a semantic baseline for task alignment with a structure-aware pipeline that discovers query-related model-card tables using table discovery operators such as unionability, joinability, and keyword search. Retrieved tables are mapped back to model cards under a controlled top-k budget, enabling fair comparison between text-based and table-based retrieval. Beyond retrieval, StructuredSemanticSearch adapts table integration to the model-table domain through orientation-aware integration, producing compact integrated views of tables from partially overlapping and sometimes transposed evidence tables. For evaluation, we introduce a nugget-based, auditable protocol that extracts compact evidence items from model cards, matches queries to condition- or intent-specific nuggets, and measures evidence coverage and diversity over retrieved model-card candidate sets. This protocol also provides a scalable path toward approximate, evidence-based labeling in dynamic model lakes. Experiments on 597 model-recommendation queries show improved nugget coverage for the structure-aware pipeline than semantic baseline

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Near-Optimal Generalized Private Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21601)
*Anamay Chaturvedi,Monika Henzinger,Jalaj Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义阈值机制（GTM），解决了差分隐私中的广义私有测试问题，通过黑盒归约实现了对 DP-CO 问题的优化，并支持自适应阈值，具有近最优的性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义私有测试问题，该问题推广了经典的稀疏向量技术（SVT），旨在实现差分隐私下的序列决策，同时优化准确度、样本复杂度和对不同机制的适用性。

Method: 提出了通用阈值机制（GTM），它是一种纯 $\varepsilon$-差分隐私的机制，能够处理任意 $(\varepsilon_t, \delta_t)$-DP 黑盒机制 $M_t$ 的序列，并支持自适应的接受阈值。

Result: GTM 实现了纯 $\varepsilon$-差分隐私；给出了具体的准确度边界 $\bar{p}_t$ 和样本复杂度分析；证明了上述指标的近最优性；并实现了从连续观察（CO）设置到批处理设置的黑盒归约。

Conclusion: 提出了广义阈值机制（GTM），证明了其在广义私有测试问题上的近最优性，并将其应用于差分隐私连续观察（DP-CO）优化及自适应超参数优化。

Abstract: In differential privacy (DP), the generalized private testing problem was introduced by Liu and Talwar (STOC 2019). Given a dataset $X \in \mathcal{X}$ and a sequence of black-box $\varepsilon_t$-DP mechanisms $M_t:\mathcal{X}\to\{+1,-1\}$, the analyst must accept the first mechanism whose success probability $p_t=\Pr[M_t(X)=+1]$ exceeds a given threshold $p^*\in(0,1)$, while achieving DP. Accuracy is measured by the gap between $p^*$ and a rejection threshold $\bar{p}$, such that with probability $1-β$ for all $t\geq1$, if $p_t\leq\bar{p}$, then $M_t$ is rejected, and if $p_t\geq p^*$, then it is accepted. This generalizes the standard private testing problem, whose solution, the Sparse Vector Technique, is ubiquitous in DP.
  We introduce the Generalized Thresholding Mechanism (GTM) for generalized private testing. For $\varepsilon>0$ and any sequence of $(\varepsilon_t,δ_t)$-DP mechanisms $M_t$, the GTM is pure $\varepsilon$-DP. For $θ>0$, $γ\in(1,2]$, and $β\in(0,1)$, $\bar{p}_t=\max(p^*/γΛ_t, 1 - γΛ_t(1-p^*))-δ_t/\varepsilon_t$ for $Λ_t=(5t\ln^3(t+2))^{(2+θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}(4/β)^{(3+θ+2/θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}$. With probability $1-β$, the number of evaluations of $M_t$ is at most $O((\ln(t/β)/(γ-1)^2)\max(Λ_t/p^*,(1-p^*)^{-1}))$ for all $t\geq 1$. Our lower bounds prove near-optimality of our accuracy and sample complexity guarantees.
  Via the GTM, we give a black-box reduction for DP optimization from the continual observation (CO) setting to the batch setting. This gives us the first DP-CO algorithms for many maximization problems. Further, the GTM permits an adaptive choice of acceptance thresholds $(p^*_t)_{t\geq1}$, addressing a challenge mentioned in prior work on using generalized private testing for hyperparameter optimization (Papernot and Steinke (ICLR 2022)).

</details>


### [278] [Finding a Solution to the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv Theorem in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21753)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种寻找Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理中目标子序列的确定性算法，通过算术级数表示技巧，将计算复杂度降低至最优的 O(n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv (EGZ) 定理中子序列查找问题，既往的最佳算法复杂度为 O(n log log log n)，作者旨在进一步降低该问题的计算复杂度至最优的线性时间 O(n)。

Method: 提出了一种针对于素数模下的“目标子集和问题”的线性时间算法。该算法利用算术级数（Arithmetic Progression）的紧凑表示来维护可达和，通过处理级数交集与Frobenius区间合并来更新表示。最后，通过标准的乘法约简技术，将该针对素数模的算法扩展至任意模数 n。

Result: 实现了寻找EGZ定理中满足和为n的倍数的n长度子序列的确定性线性时间算法。

Conclusion: 该论文通过一种通过算术级数表示实现的高效算法，成功将Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv (EGZ) 定理的子序列查找问题优化至线性时间复杂度 O(n)。

Abstract: The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv theorem states that every sequence of 2n - 1 integers contains a subsequence of length n whose sum is divisible by n. Choi, Kang, and Lim gave a simple deterministic O(n log n) algorithm for finding such a subsequence, and Leung recently improved this to O(n log log log n). We give a deterministic linear-time algorithm. The core is a linear-time algorithm for the following prime target subset-sum problem: given p - 1 nonzero residues in Z_p and a target residue, find a subset with the prescribed sum. Our algorithm maintains a compact arithmetic-progression representation of reachable sums. When two progressions intersect, a bounded Frobenius interval in their sum allows them to be merged into one longer progression, with enough growth to pay for the update. When the representation either contains a full progression or covers all nonzero residues, the target residue is recovered constructively. The standard multiplicative reduction then extends the prime algorithm to arbitrary moduli.

</details>


### [279] [An $Ω(n \log n)$ Randomized Lower Bound for Cutting a Cake into Proportionally Fair Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21829)
*Stephen Arndt,Kirk Pruhs,Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究证明，在 Robertson-Webb 模型下，为了实现比例公平，任何随机蛋糕切割算法至少需要 Ω(n log n) 次询问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究蛋糕切割问题中实现“比例公平”（Proportional Fairness）所需的计算资源下限，特别是随机算法在 Robertson-Webb 模型下的查询复杂度。

Method: 在 Robertson-Webb 查询模型下，对比例公平准则进行算法复杂性分析，通过信息论或对抗性论证推导下界。

Result: 确立了与确定性算法相同的 Ω(n log n) 查询复杂度的下界，表明随机性并不能降低在标准查询模型下达到比例公平所需的询问次数。

Conclusion: 任何实现比例公平的随机蛋糕切割算法都需要至少 Ω(n log n) 次询问，证明了该类问题的下界。

Abstract: We consider the classic cake cutting problem in the Robertson-Webb model, with the objective of proportional fairness. We show that any randomized algorithm must use $Ω(n \log n)$ queries.

</details>


### [280] [Optimal $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$-Approximation of the Permanent of Positive Semidefinite Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21946)
*Nima Anari,Farzam Ebrahimnejad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文证明了埃尔米特半正定矩阵积和式确定性多项式时间近似的最优指数界为 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$，结合了 Wick 积分与凹优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定针对埃尔米特半正定矩阵的积和式在确定性多项式时间内的最佳近似比。

Method: 利用熵论证（entropy argument）应用于积和式的 Wick 积分公式，证明了积和式被上下界 $\widehat P(A)$ 及 $e^{-\gamma n}\widehat P(A)$ 夹逼。由于 $\widehat P(A)$ 的最大化问题是凹的，因此可在多项式时间内求解。

Result: 证明了确定性多项式时间的 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$ 近似比是理论上的最优极限（假设 $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$）。

Conclusion: 该论文确定了埃尔米特半正定矩阵积和式（permanent）确定性多项式时间近似的最优指数近似比为 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$，其中 $\gamma$ 为欧拉-马斯刻若尼常数。

Abstract: We determine, up to lower-order terms in the exponent, the best possible deterministic polynomial-time approximation ratio for the permanent of a Hermitian positive semidefinite matrix. If $A\succeq 0$ has no zero diagonal entry, $d=\operatorname{rank}(A)$, $A=VV^\dagger$ with $V\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times d}$ full column rank, and $v_1,\ldots,v_n$ are the rows of $V$, define \[
  Φ(V)=\max_{X\succ 0}
  \left\{\sum_{i=1}^n \log(v_i^\dagger Xv_i)+\log\det X-\operatorname{tr} X+d\right\},
  \qquad
  \widehat P(A)=e^{Φ(V)}. \] We prove the exact sandwich \[
  e^{-γn}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A). \] Here $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant. Since the maximization is concave, this gives a deterministic polynomial-time $e^{(γ+\varepsilon)n}$-approximation for every $\varepsilon>0$. Combined with the previous $e^{(γ-\varepsilon)n}$-hardness of approximation for positive semidefinite permanents, this resolves the optimal exponential approximation ratio for deterministic polynomial-time algorithms as $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$, assuming $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$. The proof is an entropy argument applied to the standard Wick integral formula for $\operatorname{per}(A)$; the loss is exactly $γ$ per factor because $\mathbb{E}[\log T]=-γ$ for $T\sim\operatorname{Exp}(1)$.
  The result was obtained through interactions with GPT 5.5 Pro Extended: the first author's interaction was one-shot, while the second author's was a separate multi-turn interaction with high-level guidance. Both authors verified the theorem and proof. Codex was used to assemble and typeset the manuscript.

</details>


### [281] [A Coalgebraic Dijkstra Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22149)
*Takahiro Sanada,Yoàv Montacute,Kittiphon Phalakarn,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的余代数框架（CSPP）来建模多种最短路径变体问题，并设计了对应的余代数Dijkstra算法，同时给出了算法正确性的精确充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Dijkstra算法变体众多，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来涵盖这些优化问题，同时也难以清晰界定“Dijkstra类加速”适用的范围。

Method: 引入了一种基于余代数理论统一框架下的“余代数最短路径问题”（CSPP），并设计了一个通用的“余代数Dijkstra算法”。

Result: 该算法能高效解决多种优化问题（包括最短路径、最大路径、最短二叉树等），且具备与经典Dijkstra算法相当的渐进时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 代数余代数（Coalgebraic）视角为Dijkstra算法提供了简洁且统一的理论基础，且提出的特定条件是确保Dijkstra类算法正确性的充要条件。

Abstract: The Dijkstra algorithm is a classical method for solving the shortest path problem on weighted graphs. There are several variations of the Dijkstra algorithm, including algorithms for the widest path problem and for two-player games. In this paper, we introduce the coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP), a unifying framework for a broad class of optimization problems on state-transition systems. This framework encompasses not only the aforementioned problems but also new ones such as the shortest binary tree problem. We further present a coalgebraic Dijkstra algorithm for solving the CSPP efficiently under a suitable condition. Our condition is necessary and sufficient for the algorithm to return correct solutions, thereby providing a precise criterion for when Dijkstra-style acceleration is possible. We also show that the proposed algorithm achieves asymptotic complexity comparable to that of the classical Dijkstra algorithm.

</details>


### [282] [The Secretary Problem with a Stochastic Precursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22653)
*Franziska Eberle,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在线算法中一种新的预测范式：仅靠预测信号的“到达时刻”（而非内容）即可显著提升秘书问题的最优策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线算法中，预测通常被视为“建议内容”（如估值或推荐），本文旨在探索“预测触发时间本身”是否具备信息熵价值，进而能否辅助优化在线决策。

Method: 研究了在“随机顺序”和“对抗顺序”模型下，基于无内容但在最佳项到达之前触发的“随机时序先导信号”的最优停止策略。通过数学建模分析该信号触发时间对算法成功概率的影响。

Result: 在随机顺序模型中，即使是简单的均匀时序先导信号也能将成功概率从经典的 1/e 提升至至少 1/2；随着信号触发表征向后推移，成功率可趋近于 1。在对抗顺序模型中，集中的先导信号也能实现常数级的成功保证。

Conclusion: 时序信号（即使不包含实际内容）是在线决策的一个强大且独特的建议形式。研究不仅证明了时序作为预测手段的有效性，还为解决秘书问题提供了超越经典基准（1/e）的新范式。

Abstract: In learning-augmented online algorithms, predictions are usually valued for what they say: a value estimate, a solution, or an algorithmic recommendation. This paper shows that predictions can also be valuable solely due to their arrival time. We study the fundamental secretary problem augmented with a stochastic precursor: a content-free signal that is guaranteed to arrive no later than the best item, but is otherwise stochastically timed. The signal does not carry any additional information; nevertheless, its timing alone changes the structure of optimal stopping. We characterize optimal policies in the random-order and adversarial-order models. In random order, a single uniformly timed precursor already gives success probability at least $\frac12$, improving on the classic $\frac1e$ benchmark. With increasingly late precursors, the success probability approaches $1$. In adversarial order, for which traditional models do not admit strong guarantees, sufficiently concentrated precursors recover constant success guarantees. Our results show that such novel forms of asynchronous temporal information are a distinct and powerful form of advice in online decision making and may also be effective for other problems.

</details>


### [283] [On the Parameterized Complexity of Min-Sum-Radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22804)
*Pankaj Kumar,Haiko Müller,Sebastian Ordyniak,Melanie Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小半径之和（MSR）聚类问题在图度量空间下的参数化复杂度，证明了其在多种结构参数组合下是 W[1]-hard，并确定了其在 (treewidth + Delta) 参数下是 FPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSR问题在多种度量空间下已被证明是 NP-hard 的。尽管存在基于treewidth的 XP 算法，但此前尚不清楚该问题是否允许更优的参数化复杂性（如 FPT），本文旨在填补加权图度量空间下 MSR 参数化复杂度分析的空白。

Method: 使用了参数化复杂度的分析方法，包括确定 W[1]-hard hardness reduction（归约），以及利用已知的 XP 算法结合附加参数（如 Delta）证明 FPT 性质。针对加权图和无权图、各类结构参数（treewidth, vertex cover 等）进行了分类讨论。

Result: 1. 证明了在加权二分图上，MSR 对于参数 (k, Delta) 是 W[1]-hard 的；2. 否定了 MSR 在 treewidth 参数下是 FPT 的可能性，证明了即使在加权二分图上针对 (vertex cover + k) 依然是 W[1]-hard；3. 在稠密图（cliques, complete bipartite）上，针对 (k + Delta) 依然是 W[1]-hard；4. 证明了在 (treewidth + Delta) 参数下，MSR 是 FPT 的。

Conclusion: MSR问题在加权图上的参数化复杂度是复杂的：它在参数 (k, Delta)、(vertex cover + k) 以及 (k + Delta) 上是 W[1]-hard 的；但在 (treewidth + Delta) 参数下是 FPT 的。此外，该研究否定了关于treewidth参数下存在FPT算法（仅依赖treewidth）的猜测。

Abstract: In the Min-Sum-Radii (MSR) clustering problem, we are given a finite set X of n points in a metric space. The objective is to find at most k clusters centered at a subset of these points such that every point of X is assigned to one of the clusters, minimizing the sum of the radii of the clusters. The problem is known to be NP-hard even on metrics induced by weighted planar graphs and metrics with constant doubling dimension, as shown by Gibson et al. (SWAT 2008). In this work, we investigate the parameterized complexity of MSR on metrics induced by undirected graphs. We distinguish between weighted graph metrics (with positive edge weights) and unweighted graph metrics (where all edges have unit weight).
  Weighted Graph Metrics: We show that MSR is W[1]-hard on metrics induced by weighted bipartite graphs, when parameterized by the combined parameter k (the number of clusters) and Delta (the cost of the clustering). We then investigate the structural parameterized complexity of the problem. Drexler et al. (arXiv:2310.02130) showed that the MSR problem admits an XP algorithm on metrics induced by weighted graphs when parameterized by treewidth, and asked whether this can be improved to fixed-parameter tractability. We first answer their question in the negative, and more strongly show that MSR stays W[1]-hard on metrics induced by undirected weighted bipartite graphs when parameterized by the vertex cover number plus k. We then turn our attention to parameters for dense graphs and show that MSR remains W[1]-hard when parameterized by k+Delta even on cliques and complete bipartite graphs. On the positive side, we employ the known XP algorithm parameterized by treewidth, to show that the MSR problem is FPT when parameterized by the parameter treewidth plus Delta.

</details>


### [284] [Optimal Testing of Reed-Muller Codes with an Online Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22813)
*Esty Kelman,Uri Meir,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对在线擦除模型，提出了“半样本基础测试器”，实现了Reed-Muller码的最优测试，并将其应用范围扩展到了提升仿射不变码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在Kalemaj等人提出的“在线擦除模型”（online-erasure model）下，Reed-Muller码属性测试的挑战。在该模型中，查询后的对手擦除机制会导致传统测试器模式失效，因此需要设计鲁棒的测试方法。

Method: 定义并分析了“半样本基础测试器”（semi-sample-based testers）。该测试器结合了样本测试器（随机查询）和标准测试器（自由选择查询）的特点，具体运作方式为：先选择域的一个子集S，然后在S中进行均匀随机查询。

Result: 证明了半样本测试器对于Reed-Muller码的有效性，给出了其鲁棒性的最优分析，并超越了Minzer和Zheng (SODA 2024) 的先前研究结果。此外，成功将该方法拓展至提升仿射不变码。

Conclusion: 半样本测试器在在线擦除模型中对Reed-Muller码进行测试是有效的，且在该模型下实现了最优查询复杂度。该方法还推广到了提升仿射不变码（lifted affine-invariant codes），提供了此类编码在在线擦除模型下的首个测试器。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to property testing in the online-erasure model of Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, and Varma (ITCS 2022 and Theory of Computing 2023), we define and analyze {\em semi-sample-based testers} for Reed-Muller codes. The task in Reed-Muller testing is to determine whether an input function $f: \F^n \to \F$ belongs to the Reed-Muller code or is far from it, using as few point queries to $f$ as possible. Reed-Muller testing is a well-studied task with its roots in both the Property Testing and Probabilistically Checkable Proofs literature. The online-erasure model introduces a twist: after each query made, an adversary may erase up to $t$ points of the input function, potentially thwarting any test in which the queries follow a predictable pattern.
  Semi-sample-based testers are a hybrid between sample-based testers -- which can only make uniformly random queries to the input function -- and standard testers, which can choose their queries freely. They are designed with the online-erasure model in mind and operate by first choosing some subset $S$ of the domain and then making their queries uniformly at random inside of $S$. We describe semi-sample-based testers for the Reed-Muller code and give an optimal analysis of their soundness.
  Consequently, we show that semi-sample-based testers are indeed effective in the presence of online erasures, and thereby achieve optimal query complexity for testing the Reed-Muller code in the online-erasure model. This result improves upon prior work of Minzer and Zheng (SODA 2024). As an added bonus, we show that semi-sample-based testers also exist for the lifted affine-invariant codes of Guo, Kopparty, and Sudan (ITCS 2013), thereby providing the first known testers for these codes in the online-erasure model.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [285] [DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22781)
*Yunpeng Dong,Jingkai He,Yuze Hou,Dong Du,Zhonghu Xu,Si Yu,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 针对AI智能体高频状态探索中因全量检查点造成的性能瓶颈，本文提出了DeltaBox，通过增量式快照（DeltaFS和DeltaCR）将C/R延迟大幅降低至毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检查点/回滚（C/R）机制在AI智能体的高频状态探索场景下，由于全量复制导致高延迟（数百毫秒至数秒），严重制约了复杂任务中的深度搜索与大规模并发探索。

Method: 提出DeltaState OS级抽象，包括：1) DeltaFS：利用分层文件系统与CoW（写时复制）机制实现文件状态的增量管理；2) DeltaCR：通过增量快照和基于冻结模板进程的直接fork()机制实现进程状态的快速恢复。两者共同构成了DeltaBox沙箱。

Result: 在SWE-bench和RL微基准测试中，DeltaBox将检查点创建和回滚的性能分别优化至14ms和5ms，在固定时间预算内显著提升了AI智能体的搜索探索能力。

Conclusion: DeltaBox通过DeltaState抽象与两个核心OS机制（DeltaFS和DeltaCR），成功将AI智能体沙箱的检查点和回滚延迟从秒级/百毫秒级降低至毫秒级（14ms/5ms），有效解决了LLM智能体在深度搜索和大规模扩展时的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: LLM-powered AI agents require high-frequency state exploration (e.g., test-time tree search and reinforcement learning), relying on rapid checkpoint and rollback (C/R) of the complete sandbox state, including files and process state (e.g., memory, contexts, etc.). Existing mechanisms duplicate the entire state, causing hundreds of milliseconds to seconds of latency per C/R, which severely bottlenecks deep search and large-scale fan-outs.
  This paper observes that subsequent checkpoints in AI agents are highly similar. Therefore, instead of full duplication, a sandbox should only duplicate the changes between consecutive checkpoints (Key Insight). However, it is non-trivial to realize the idea, mainly due to the missing OS supports. This paper proposes a new OS-level abstraction, DeltaState, to enable the change-based transactional C/R for AI agents with two co-designed OS mechanisms. First, DeltaFS enables change-based filesystem C/R by organizing the file states into layers and dynamically freezing the writable layer and inserting a new one during checkpoint, reducing file updates to copy-on-write, and making rollback a simple layer switch. Second, DeltaCR enables change-based process state C/R using incremental dumps, and accelerates rollback by bypassing traditional pipelines to directly fork() from a frozen template process. We then present DeltaBox, a novel agent sandbox achieving millisecond level C/R through the two new mechanisms. Evaluations on SWE-bench and RL micro-benchmarks show DeltaBox completes checkpoint and rollback in millisecond-level latency (14ms and 5ms, respectively), empowering agents to explore substantially more nodes under fixed time budgets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [CompPow: A Case for Component-level GPU Power Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21847)
*Shaizeen Aga,Mohamed Assem Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CompPow方法，通过实现GPU内部的组件感知功率管理，验证了在不牺牲性能的前提下提升GPU能效及性能的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML对GPU需求的爆发式增长，GPU功耗已成为ML数据中心的主要负担；现有研究多关注数据中心/集群层面的功耗，缺乏对GPU内部组件级功耗管理的研究。

Method: 提出了一种称为“CompPow”的组件感知（Component-awareness）方法，通过深入分析GPU内部各集成组件的功耗特性来进行精细化的功率管理。

Result: 在多种ML操作和执行模式下，CompPow能够将能效提升10%，并将性能提升5%。

Conclusion: 组件感知的软硬件协同设计是提升现代GPU能效和性能的关键路径，未来应进一步深入研究以挖掘更多节能潜力。

Abstract: The ever increasing demand for ML-driven intelligence in a wide spectrum of domains has led to ubiquity of GPUs. At the same time, GPUs are notorious for their power consumption needs and often dominate power allocation in a typical ML datacenter. While datacenter-level power optimizations which focus on collection of GPUs are promising, in this work, we take a different tack -- namely, we take a closer look at power consumption inside a GPU. Specifically, as modern GPUs are comprised of integrated components, we make a case for component-awareness, termed CompPow in this work, for improved power management in modern GPUs. We demonstrate for a variety of ML operations and execution patterns, CompPow has the potential to deliver higher energy efficiency (10%) and even improved performance (5%). We conclude with recommendations on how component-aware software-hardware co-design can extract additional energy efficiency from modern GPUs.

</details>


### [287] [Emerging memory technologies at room/cryogenic temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21912)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向高性能计算需求，在从室温到低温环境下运行的多种易失性与非易失性存储技术，详细评估了各技术的物理特性、性能边界及在未来计算架构中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着传统工艺微缩受限，计算系统在延迟、功耗、数据移动开销等方面面临巨大挑战，且机器学习等新兴工作负载对高带宽、低延迟和高密度存储的需求日益增长，亟需全面梳理从室温到低温环境下的各类存储技术方案。

Method: 文献综述法。通过对各类存储技术（包括SRAM、DRAM、eDRAM、闪存、新型非易失性存储器如RRAM、MRAM、FeFET）以及特定于低温环境的存储电路（如UTBB-SOI、JJFET）的运作原理、读写机制、保留特性进行分类比较与系统性分析。

Result: 全面构建了室温与低温存储技术的对比框架。明确了各技术在面积、性能、可扩展性和能源效率方面的折中（Trade-offs），并指出了适用于高性能计算及超低温量子计算系统的不同技术路径及其挑战。

Conclusion: 当前的内存技术正处于向高性能、高能效及低温环境（如量子计算）演进的关键时期。通过综合评估室温与低温存储技术的折衷方案，未来的架构设计需要在存储密度、功耗、性能和可扩展性之间寻找新的平衡点，以突破传统计算的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: As conventional technology scaling approaches physical and power limitations, modern computing systems increasingly face performance bottlenecks arising from memory latency, energy consumption, scalability constraints, and data movement overheads. Simultaneously, emerging workloads such as machine learning, graph analytics, and scientific computing demand memory technologies with higher bandwidth, lower latency, improved energy efficiency, and greater storage density. These challenges have motivated extensive research into both room-temperature memories and cryogenic memory systems targeted toward superconducting and quantum computing platforms.
  This chapter presents an overview of volatile and non-volatile memory technologies operating across room-temperature and cryogenic environments. The discussion includes SRAM, DRAM, embedded DRAM (eDRAM), NAND/NOR Flash, Resistive Random Access Memory (RRAM), Magneto-resistive Random Access Memory (MRAM), and Ferroelectric Field-Effect Transistor (FeFET)-based memories. In addition, cryogenic technologies including UTBB-SOI-based pseudo-static storage circuits and Josephson Junction Field-Effect Transistor (JJFET)-based devices are discussed in the context of ultra-low-temperature computing systems. The chapter highlights the operational principles, read/write mechanisms, retention behavior, and tradeoffs among area, performance, scalability, and energy efficiency across these memory technologies, while examining challenges and opportunities for future room-temperature and cryogenic computing architectures.

</details>
